      if (isPhone() && PICKS.size === 1) $("setup").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    };
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
}

function renderSetup() {
  const none = YEAR === 5 && !PICKS.size;
  $("setup").classList.toggle("empty", none); $("start").disabled = none;
  if (none) { $("setupTitle").textContent = "Pick a topic"; $("setupNote").textContent = "Tap one or more topics above to practise them."; $("types").innerHTML = ""; $("levels").hidden = true; return; }
  const many = YEAR === 5 && PICKS.size > 1;
  $("setupTitle").textContent = many ? picksLabel(PICKS, Object.keys(MAIN).length) : topic;
  const box = $("types"); box.innerHTML = "";
  $("setupNote").textContent = many ? (PICKS.size > 2 && PICKS.size < Object.keys(MAIN).length ? [...PICKS].join(" · ") + ". " : "") + "Questions from these topics, mixed together. Tap parts below to practise only those." : "Practise the whole topic, or tap one or more parts of it.";
  const lb = $("levels"); lb.innerHTML = '<span class="muted">Level:</span>';
  LEVELS.forEach(l => {
    const c = document.createElement("button");
    c.type = "button"; c.className = "chip lvl lvl-" + l.toLowerCase(); c.textContent = l; c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", l === level);
    c.onclick = () => { level = l; renderSetup(); };
    lb.appendChild(c);
  });
  lb.hidden = YEAR === 5;   /* Year 5 sets already go from easy to hard */
  if (topic === MIXED && YEAR !== 5) return;
  if (YEAR === 5) {   /* parts of every picked topic; tap several, or All */
    const parts = [...PICKS].flatMap(t => subsOf(t).filter(sub => withLevel("All", () => topicPool(t, sub).length)));
    [...PARTS].forEach(p => { if (!parts.includes(p)) PARTS.delete(p); });
    ["All", ...parts].forEach(t => {
      const c = document.createElement("button");
      c.type = "button"; c.className = "chip"; c.textContent = t; c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", t === "All" ? !PARTS.size : PARTS.has(t));
      c.onclick = () => { if (t === "All") PARTS.clear(); else if (PARTS.has(t)) PARTS.delete(t); else PARTS.add(t); type = PARTS.size === 1 ? [...PARTS][0] : "All"; renderSetup(); };
      box.appendChild(c);
    });
    return;
  }
  const types = ["All", ...subsOf(topic).filter(sub => withLevel("All", () => topicPool(topic, sub).length) || generatorsFor(topic, sub).length)];
  if (!types.includes(type)) type = "All";
  types.forEach(t => {
    const c = document.createElement("button");
    c.type = "button"; c.className = "chip"; c.textContent = t; c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", t === type);
    c.onclick = () => { type = t; renderSetup(); };
    box.appendChild(c);
  });
}

const HASH = { home: "", quiz: "practice", done: "results", starterView: "starter", profileView: "profile" };
function show(id, fromHash) {
  setView(id);
  ["home", "quiz", "done", "parents", "printView", "pastView", "raceView", "helpView", "starterView", "profileView"].forEach(s => $(s).hidden = s !== id);
  $("homebar").hidden = id !== "home"; $("todayBtn").hidden = id !== "home"; $("starterBtn").hidden = id !== "home"; if (id !== "home") { $("planLine").hidden = true; $("plan2Btn").hidden = true; } if (typeof renderAssigned === "function") renderAssigned(); if (id === "home" && typeof renderSuggest === "function") { renderSuggest(); renderToday(); applySettings(); if (typeof renderStarterHome === "function") renderStarterHome(); }
  $("leave").hidden = true;
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  if (!fromHash) {
    const h = HASH[id];
    if (("#" + h) !== location.hash && !(h === "" && !location.hash)) {
      try { if (h) history.pushState(null, "", "#" + h); else history.pushState(null, "", location.pathname + location.search); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
}

function setView(id) {
  document.body.dataset.view = id;
  document.querySelectorAll("#tabbar button").forEach(b => { if (b.dataset.tab === id) b.setAttribute("aria-current", "page"); else b.removeAttribute("aria-current"); });
}
document.querySelectorAll("#tabbar button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
  const t = b.dataset.tab;
  if (t === "home") show("home"); else if (t === "pastView") openPapers("pt"); else if (t === "profileView") $("profileBtn").click(); else openParents();
});
const isPhone = () => matchMedia("(max-width: 699px)").matches;

/* ----- report a problem with a question (and checking mode) ----- */
const checking = () => !!(store.get("settings") || {}).check;
function reportUI(id) {
  return `<div class="report">${checking() ? `<span class="qid">${stEsc(id)}</span>` : ""}<button type="button" class="linkbtn" data-report>Report a problem</button>
    <div class="rbox" hidden><textarea rows="3" placeholder="What is wrong? For example: the wording, the answer, the picture or how it looks on the screen."></textarea>
    <div class="row"><button type="button" class="primary small" data-rsave>Save</button><button type="button" class="ghost small" data-rcancel>Cancel</button><span class="muted small" data-rmsg></span></div></div></div>`.replace("<div class=\"report\">", `<div class="report" data-qid="${stEsc(id)}">`);
}
function wireReport(box) {
  const r = box.querySelector(".report"); if (!r) return;
  const rb = r.querySelector(".rbox"), ta = r.querySelector("textarea");
  r.querySelector("[data-report]").onclick = () => { rb.hidden = !rb.hidden; if (!rb.hidden) ta.focus(); };
  r.querySelector("[data-rcancel]").onclick = () => { rb.hidden = true; ta.value = ""; };
  r.querySelector("[data-rsave]").onclick = () => {
    const t = ta.value.trim(); if (!t) { ta.focus(); return; }
    const list = store.get("reports") || []; list.push({ id: r.dataset.qid, note: t.slice(0, 1000), at: Date.now(), child: childName() || "", screen: innerWidth + "×" + innerHeight });
    store.set("reports", list); ta.value = ""; rb.hidden = true; if (typeof Cloud !== "undefined") Cloud.bump(r.dataset.qid, false, true);
    r.querySelector("[data-report]").textContent = "Reported – thank you";
  };
}
function hardHTML() {
  const h = hardQuestions();
  return `<h3>Questions most often got wrong</h3><p class="muted small">On this device: questions tried 3 or more times and missed at least 7 times in 10. Worth checking the wording and the answer.</p>` +
    (h.length ? `<table class="ptable"><tr><th>ID</th><th>Question</th><th>Answer</th><th class="n">Missed</th></tr>${h.map(x => `<tr><td>${escH(x.id)}</td><td>${escH(x.q.q.slice(0, 90))}</td><td>${escH(x.q.a)}</td><td class="n">${x.wrong}/${x.tries}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>` : '<p class="muted">None yet.</p>');
}
function renderReports() {
  const list = store.get("reports") || [], box = $("pReports");
  const text = list.map(x => `${x.id}: ${x.note}`).join("\n");
  box.innerHTML = `<p class="muted small">Problems reported from the question screens. Turn on Checking mode in Settings to see each question's number while you check. Copy the list and send it to have the questions fixed.</p>
    ${list.length ? `<table class="ptable"><tr><th>Question</th><th>Problem</th><th>Date</th></tr>${list.map(x => `<tr><td><b>${stEsc(x.id)}</b></td><td>${stEsc(x.note)}</td><td class="muted small">${new Date(x.at).toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}${x.screen ? "<br>" + x.screen : ""}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>
    <div class="row"><button class="primary" id="rCopy" type="button">Copy all (${list.length})</button><button class="ghost" id="rClear" type="button">Clear the list</button><span class="muted small" id="rMsg"></span></div>
    <textarea id="rText" rows="6" readonly style="width:100%;margin-top:8px">${stEsc(text)}</textarea>` : `<p>No problems reported yet.</p>`}` + hardHTML();
  if (!list.length) return;
  $("rCopy").onclick = async () => { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); $("rMsg").textContent = "Copied."; } catch (e) { $("rText").select(); $("rMsg").textContent = "Select all the text in the box and copy it."; } };
  $("rClear").onclick = () => { if (confirm("Clear all reported problems?")) { store.set("reports", []); renderReports(); } };
}
function askLeave() { $("leave").hidden = false; $("leaveNo").focus(); }
function leaveSet() { clearInterval(window.__tick); run = null; show("home"); }

function addItems(list) { list.forEach(q => run.items.push({ q, built: buildQuestion(q) })); }

function newRun(fromMistakes) {
  run = { items: [], i: 0, score: 0, marks: 0, totalMarks: 0, answered: 0, missed: [], fromMistakes, times: [], startedAt: Date.now() };
  clearInterval(window.__tick);
  window.__tick = setInterval(() => { if (run && !$("quiz").hidden) $("qtime").textContent = fmtTime((Date.now() - run.startedAt) / 1000); }, 1000);
  $("qtime").textContent = "0:00";
}
const fmtTime = s => Math.floor(s / 60) + ":" + String(Math.round(s % 60)).padStart(2, "0");
const PAPER = {
  "Column addition": "Use paper and a pen: set this out in columns to work it out.",
  "Column subtraction": "Use paper and a pen: set this out in columns to work it out.",
  "Column multiplication": "Get a pencil and paper ready: set this out as column multiplication.",
  "Division with remainders": "Get a pencil and paper ready: set this out as a written division.",
  "Short division": "Get a pencil and paper ready: set this out as a short division.",
  "Missing digits": "Get a pencil and paper ready: write the calculation out in columns.",
  "Train stops": "Get a pencil and paper ready: write down each step.",
};
function paperHint(q) {
  if (PAPER[q.t]) return PAPER[q.t];
  if (/column|long division|short division/i.test(q.q)) return "Get a pencil and paper ready: work this one out in columns.";
  if ((q.m || 1) >= 3) return "Get a pencil and paper ready: this one has several steps.";
  return "";
}

function pickSet(n, avoidIds) {
  if (YEAR === 5) {
    const seen = new Set(store.get("seen") || []), avoid = avoidIds || new Set(), sets = {};
    pool().filter(q => !avoid.has(q.id)).forEach(q => (sets[q.set || q.id] = sets[q.set || q.id] || []).push(q));
    const groups = Object.values(sets), fresh = shuffle(groups.filter(g => !g.some(q => seen.has(q.id)))), old = shuffle(groups.filter(g => g.some(q => seen.has(q.id))));
    const order = fresh.concat(old), bySk = {}; order.forEach(g => (bySk[g[0].skill] = bySk[g[0].skill] || []).push(g));
    const lanes = shuffle(Object.values(bySk)), out = [];   /* take turns between the parts of a topic */
    for (let i = 0; out.length < n && lanes.some(l => l.length); i++) { const l = lanes[i % lanes.length]; if (l.length) out.push(...l.shift()); }
    return out.slice(0, Math.max(n, 1));
  }
  const all = pool(), seen = new Set(store.get("seen") || []), avoid = avoidIds || new Set();
  const gensHere = generatorsFor(topic, type).length;
  const bank = shuffle(all.filter(q => !seen.has(q.id) && !avoid.has(q.id))).slice(0, gensHere ? Math.ceil(n * 0.7) : n);
  let list = bank.concat(makeGenerated(n - bank.length, topic, type, bank.map(q => q.q)));
  if (list.length < n) {
    const used = new Set(list.map(q => q.id));
    list = list.concat(shuffle(all.filter(q => !used.has(q.id) && !avoid.has(q.id))).slice(0, n - list.length));
  }
  return shuffle(list);
}
const autoKey = () => "autoLevel:" + topic;
function withLevel(lv, f) { const keep = level; level = lv; try { return f(); } finally { level = keep; } }
let FROM_SETUP = false;
function start() {
  if (FROM_SETUP && YEAR === 5 && !PICKS.size) { $("setupNote").textContent = "Tap one or more topics above first."; return; }
  if (!hasFull() && sessionsToday() >= FREE.sessionsPerDay) { $("setupNote").textContent = `That's ${FREE.sessionsPerDay} sessions today on the free plan. A parent can open everything in Parents → Plan and access.`; return; }
  TARGET = readCount();
  newRun(false);
  if (FROM_SETUP && YEAR === 5) { run.sks = pickedSkills(); if (PICKS.size > 1 || PARTS.size > 1) run.label = PARTS.size > 1 ? picksLabel(PARTS) : picksLabel(PICKS, Object.keys(MAIN).length); }
  let list;
  if (level === AUTO) {
    const lv = store.get(autoKey()) || "Easy";
    run.auto = { lv, streak: 0, wrongs: 0 };
    list = withLevel(lv, () => pickSet(TARGET));
    if (list.length < TARGET) list = list.concat(withLevel("All", () => pickSet(TARGET - list.length, new Set(list.map(q => q.id)))));
  } else list = pickSet(TARGET);
  if (!list.length) { $("setupNote").textContent = "There are no questions here at this level yet. Pick another level or part of the topic."; return; }
  addItems(list);
  show("quiz"); renderQ();
}
function adapt(earned) {
  const A = run.auto; if (!A) return "";
  if (earned) { A.streak++; A.wrongs = 0; } else { A.wrongs++; A.streak = 0; }
  let i = STEPS.indexOf(A.lv), msg = "";
  if (A.streak >= 3 && i < STEPS.length - 1) { i++; msg = `Level up! Now trying ${STEPS[i]} questions.`; }
  else if (A.wrongs >= 2 && i > 0) { i--; msg = `Let's try some ${STEPS[i]} questions for a bit.`; }
  if (!msg) return "";
  A.lv = STEPS[i]; A.streak = 0; A.wrongs = 0; store.set(autoKey(), A.lv);
  const left = run.items.length - run.i - 1;
  if (left > 0) {
    const used = new Set(run.items.map(x => x.q.id));
    const fresh = withLevel(A.lv, () => pickSet(left, used));
    if (fresh.length) { run.items.splice(run.i + 1); addItems(fresh); }
  }
  return msg;
}

function startMistakes(missed) {
  newRun(true);
  let list = missed.slice();
  missed.forEach(q => { list = list.concat(makeFrom(GEN[q.tab] && GEN[q.tab][q.t] ? [[q.tab, q.t]] : [], 2, list.map(x => x.q))); });
  addItems(shuffle(list.slice(0, 50)));
  show("quiz"); renderQ();
}

/* lines written "Station | Train A | Train B" are shown as a proper table (timetables, price lists) */
function splitTable(lines) {
  const rows = lines.filter(l => l.includes(" | ")), other = lines.filter(l => !l.includes(" | "));
  return rows.length >= 2 ? { rows, other } : null;
}
function tableHTML(rows) {
  const cells = r => r.split(" | ").map(c => fracHTML(c.trim()));
  const [h, ...b] = rows;
  return `<div class="ttwrap"><table class="tt"><thead><tr>${cells(h).map(c => `<th scope="col">${c}</th>`).join("")}</tr></thead><tbody>${b.map(r => `<tr>${cells(r).map((c, i) => i ? `<td>${c}</td>` : `<th scope="row">${c}</th>`).join("")}</tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function renderQ() {
  if (run.i >= run.items.length) return finish();
  const total = run.items.length;
  $("qcount").textContent = (run.checkup ? "Check-up · " : run.paperName ? run.paperName + " · " : run.mock ? "Mock test · " : "") + `Question ${run.i + 1} of ${total}` + (run.auto ? ` · ${run.auto.lv}` : "");
  $("qscore").textContent = `${run.marks} ${run.marks === 1 ? "mark" : "marks"}`;
  $("bar").style.width = (run.i / total * 100) + "%";
  $("feedback").hidden = true; $("next").hidden = true;
  $("qreport").innerHTML = reportUI(run.items[run.i].q.id); wireReport($("qreport"));
  $("skip").hidden = run.i >= run.items.length - 1;
  const { q, built } = run.items[run.i];
  const [first, ...rest] = built.text.split("\n");
  let shownText = /\ba\) /.test(first) ? first.replace(/\s+(?=[a-d]\) )/g, "\n") : first;
  const calc = shownText.match(/^([^\n]*?[:.])\s+((?:[−\d(IVXLC]|___)[^:\n]*(?:=|___)[^:\n]*)$/);
  if (calc) shownText = calc[1] + "\n" + calc[2];
  if (false) { // automatic "task on a new line" switched off (18 Nov): questions show as written
    const task = shownText.match(/^(.*[.!?])\s+((?:What|How|Which|Who|If|Is|Are|Can|Does|Do|Will|Write|Work|Round|Give|Find|Estimate|Use|Fill|Choose)\b[^.?!]*[?.])$/);
    if (task) shownText = task[1] + "\n" + task[2];
  }
  const tbl = splitTable(rest);
  if (tbl) rest.splice(0, rest.length);
  $("qtext").innerHTML = fracHTML(shownText).replace(/\n/g, "<br>") + (tbl ? tableHTML(tbl.rows) + tbl.other.map(l => `<span class="ttafter">${fracHTML(l)}</span>`).join("") : "");
  if (run.items[run.i].skipped) $("qtext").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", '<span class="muted" style="display:block;font-size:.85em">You skipped this one earlier – have a go now.</span>');
  const seq = $("qseq"); seq.innerHTML = ""; seq.hidden = !rest.length;
  rest.join("   ").split(/\s{3,}/).filter(Boolean).forEach(t => { const sp = document.createElement("span"); sp.innerHTML = fracHTML(t); if (t === "___") sp.className = "blank"; seq.appendChild(sp); });
  const mk = q.m || 1; $("qmarks").textContent = mk === 1 ? "1 mark" : mk + " marks";
  $("qpic").innerHTML = drawPic(q.pic); $("qpic").hidden = !q.pic;
  const paper = paperHint(q); $("qpaper").textContent = paper; $("qpaper").hidden = !paper;
  const it = run.items[run.i]; run.shownAt = it.shownAt || (it.shownAt = Date.now());
  const box = $("options"); box.innerHTML = "";
  if (built.order) {
    box.className = "ordering";
    box.innerHTML = `<p class="muted">Tap them in order. Tap one again to put it back.</p><div class="picked" id="orderPicked"></div><div class="pool" id="orderPool"></div><div class="row"><button class="primary" id="orderCheck" type="button" disabled>Check</button><button class="ghost" id="orderClear" type="button">Start again</button></div>`;
    const picked = [], pool = built.items.slice();
    const draw = () => {
      const mk = (txt, onTap, cls) => { const b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "tile " + cls; b.innerHTML = fracHTML(txt); b.onclick = onTap; return b; };
      $("orderPicked").innerHTML = ""; $("orderPool").innerHTML = "";
      picked.forEach((x, i) => { if (i) { const s = document.createElement("span"); s.className = "arrowsep"; s.textContent = "→"; $("orderPicked").append(s); } $("orderPicked").append(mk(x, () => { const k = picked.indexOf(x); if (k < 0) return; picked.splice(k, 1); pool.push(x); draw(); }, "on")); });
      if (!picked.length) $("orderPicked").innerHTML = '<span class="muted">Your order will appear here</span>';
      pool.forEach(x => $("orderPool").append(mk(x, () => { const k = pool.indexOf(x); if (k < 0) return; pool.splice(k, 1); picked.push(x); draw(); }, "off")));
      $("orderCheck").disabled = pool.length > 0;
    };
    $("orderCheck").onclick = () => answer("order", picked.join(" · "));
    $("orderClear").onclick = () => { pool.push(...picked.splice(0)); draw(); };
    $("qseq").hidden = true;
    draw();
    return;
  }
  if (built.typed) {
    box.className = "typed";
    const pre = q.a.startsWith("£") ? "£" : "";
    const sm = q.a.match(/(p|°C| ?(?:m|km|cm|mm|g|kg|ml|l)| minutes?| seconds| days| years| sheets| minibuses)$/);
    const suf = sm && !/^\d+:\d{2}/.test(q.a) ? sm[1].trim() : "";
    const core = q.a.replace(/^£/, "").replace(sm && suf ? sm[1] : "", "");
    const mode = /[a-z:\/ ]/i.test(core) ? "text" : "decimal";
    const fm = core.trim().match(/^(?:(\d+) )?(\d+)\/(\d+)$/);
    if (fm) {
      box.innerHTML = `<label class="muted">Type your answer: top number, then bottom number</label><div class="row typed-row fracin">${fm[1] ? '<input id="fW" type="text" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="Whole number" class="fw">' : ""}<span class="fracbox"><input id="fN" type="text" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="Top number"><span class="fline"></span><input id="fD" type="text" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="Bottom number"></span><input id="typedAnswer" type="hidden"><button class="primary" id="checkTyped" type="button">Check</button></div>`;
      const ins = [...box.querySelectorAll(".fracin input:not([type=hidden])")];
      ins.forEach((el, i) => {
        el.addEventListener("input", () => { el.value = el.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); });
        el.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key !== "Enter") return; e.preventDefault(); if (e.repeat) return; if (i < ins.length - 1 && !ins[i + 1].value) ins[i + 1].focus(); else go(); };
      });
      const go = () => {
        const w = $("fW") ? $("fW").value.trim() : "", n = $("fN").value.trim(), d = $("fD").value.trim();
        if (!n || !d) { (n ? $("fD") : $("fN")).focus(); return; }
        answer(null, (w ? w + " " : "") + n + "/" + d);
      };
      $("checkTyped").onclick = go;
      ins[0].focus();
      return;
    }
    box.innerHTML = `<label for="typedAnswer" class="muted">Type your answer</label><div class="row typed-row">${pre ? `<span class="unit">${pre}</span>` : ""}<input id="typedAnswer" type="text" autocomplete="off" inputmode="${mode}">${suf ? `<span class="unit">${suf}</span>` : ""}<button class="primary" id="checkTyped" type="button">Check</button></div>`;
    const inp = $("typedAnswer");
    const allowed = /^[IVXLC]+$/i.test(core) ? /[^ivxlc]/gi
      : /[ap]m$/i.test(core) ? /[^0-9:. apm]/gi
      : /:/.test(core) ? /[^0-9:.]/g
      : /\//.test(core) ? /[^0-9\/ ]/g
      : /[^0-9.,\-−]/g;
    inp.addEventListener("input", () => { const v = inp.value.replace(allowed, ""); if (v !== inp.value) { inp.value = v; inp.classList.add("nudge"); setTimeout(() => inp.classList.remove("nudge"), 400); } });
    const go = () => { if (inp.value.trim()) answer(null, inp.value); };
    $("checkTyped").onclick = go;
    inp.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); if (!e.repeat) go(); } };
    inp.focus();
    return;
  }
  box.className = "options" + (q.optCol || built.options.some(o => o.length > 14 || /\ba\)/.test(o)) ? " long" : "");
  built.options.forEach(o => {
    const b = document.createElement("button");
    b.type = "button"; b.className = "opt"; b.innerHTML = fracHTML(o); b.dataset.value = o;
    if (o.includes(" · ")) { b.textContent = ""; o.split(" · ").forEach((part, i) => { if (i) b.append(/order/i.test(q.q) ? " → " : " and "); const sp = document.createElement("span"); sp.className = "nw"; sp.innerHTML = fracHTML(part); b.append(sp); }); b.dataset.value = o; }
    b.onclick = () => answer(b, o);
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
}

function answer(btn, choice) {
  const { q } = run.items[run.i];
  const typed = btn === null || btn === undefined, ordered = btn === "order";
  let ok = typed ? norm(choice) === norm(q.a) : choice === q.a;
  if (!ok && typed && /^\d{1,2}:\d\d$/.test(q.a.trim())) { const x = String(choice).trim().match(/^(\d{1,2})[:.](\d\d)$/), y = q.a.trim().match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d\d)$/); if (x && y && x[2] === y[2] && (+x[1]) % 12 === (+y[1]) % 12) ok = true; }
  if (!ok && typed && /^(Adding|Subtracting), same denominator$/.test(q.t) && !/simplest|mixed|improper/i.test(q.q)) {
    const x = String(choice).trim().match(/^(\d+)\/(\d+)$/), y = q.a.match(/^(\d+)\/(\d+)$/);
    if (x && y && +x[2] > 0 && +x[1] * +y[2] === +y[1] * +x[2]) ok = true;
  }
  const item = run.items[run.i];
  if (!ok && !run.mock && !item.tried) {
    item.tried = true;
    renderQ();
    const fb = $("feedback"); fb.hidden = false; fb.className = "feedback bad retry-note"; fb.innerHTML = "<strong>Not quite – have another go!</strong>" + (S().hints === false ? "" : hintFor(q));
    [...$("options").children].forEach(b => { if ((b.dataset.value || b.textContent) === choice) { b.disabled = true; b.classList.add("wrong"); } });
    return;
  }
  run.times.push(Math.min(600, (Date.now() - run.shownAt) / 1000));
  const firstGo = !item.tried, earned = ok && firstGo;
  qStat(q.id, earned);
  (run.log = run.log || []).push({ tab: q.tab, t: q.t, ok: earned });
  reviewMark(q, earned);
  if (ordered) { document.querySelectorAll("#options .tile, #orderCheck, #orderClear").forEach(b => b.disabled = true); $("orderPicked").classList.add(ok ? "right" : "wrong"); }
  if (typed) { const inp = $("typedAnswer"); inp.disabled = true; $("checkTyped").disabled = true; inp.classList.add(ok ? "right" : "wrong"); document.querySelectorAll(".fracin input").forEach(i => { i.disabled = true; i.classList.add(ok ? "right" : "wrong"); }); }
  run.answered++; run.totalMarks += (q.m || 1); if (earned) run.marks += (q.m || 1);
  if (!q.gen) { const seen = new Set(store.get("seen") || []); seen.add(q.id); store.set("seen", [...seen]); }
  if (run.mock) { (run.given = run.given || {})[q.id] = { a: String(choice == null ? "" : choice), ok: earned }; if (earned) run.score++; else run.missed.push(q); run.i++; renderQ(); return; }
  const levelMsg = adapt(earned);
  [...$("options").children].forEach(b => { b.disabled = true; if ((b.dataset.value || b.textContent) === q.a) b.classList.add("right"); });
  const fb = $("feedback"); fb.hidden = false;
  if (earned) {
    run.score++; fb.className = "feedback good"; fb.textContent = "Correct, well done!";
  } else if (ok) {
    run.missed.push(q); fb.className = "feedback good"; fb.textContent = "Got it on the second go! No marks this time, but well done for sticking with it.";
  } else {
    if (btn && btn.classList) btn.classList.add("wrong"); run.missed.push(q);
    fb.className = "feedback bad";
    fb.innerHTML = `<strong></strong><span class="sol"></span>`;
    fb.querySelector("strong").innerHTML = fracHTML("Not quite. The answer is " + q.a.split(" · ").join(/order/i.test(q.q) ? ", " : " and ") + ".");
    fb.querySelector(".sol").innerHTML = fracHTML("How to work it out: " + q.s);
    const hc = helpFor(q);
    if (hc) {
      fb.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", '<div class="helpline"><button type="button" class="ghost">Help card</button></div>');
      fb.querySelector(".helpline button").onclick = e => { e.target.parentNode.outerHTML = `<div class="helpbox">${helpBack(hc)}</div>`; };
    }
  }
  if (levelMsg) fb.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", `<span class="levelmsg">${levelMsg}</span>`);
  $("qscore").textContent = `${run.marks} ${run.marks === 1 ? "mark" : "marks"}`;
  const last = run.i + 1 >= run.items.length;
  $("skip").hidden = true;
  $("next").hidden = false;
  $("next").textContent = last ? "See my score" : "Next";
  run.answeredAt = Date.now();
  setTimeout(() => $("next").focus({ preventScroll: true }), 0);
}

function finish() {
  const answered = run.answered;
  if (run.mock && run.paperName) run.totalMarks = run.items.reduce((a, x) => a + (x.q.m || 1), 0);   /* a paper is marked out of all its marks, not just the ones answered */
  const pctNow = run.totalMarks ? Math.round(run.marks / run.totalMarks * 100) : 0;
  if (!run.fromMistakes && !run.mock && answered >= MIN_FOR_BEST) {
    const best = run.label ? 101 : store.get("bestPct:" + topic + (YEAR === 4 ? "" : ":Y" + YEAR));
    if (best == null || pctNow > best) store.set("bestPct:" + topic + (YEAR === 4 ? "" : ":Y" + YEAR), pctNow);
  }
  if (run.answered) logHistory();
  if (run.today && run.answered) store.set("todayDone", dayKey(Date.now()));
  if (run.fixFor && run.answered >= Math.ceil(run.items.length * 0.8)) { const f = fixedPapers(); if (!f.includes(run.fixFor)) store.set("fixedPapers", [...f, run.fixFor]); }
  $("doneTitle").textContent = run.fixFor ? run.fixFor.replace("Practice test", "Paper") + ": fix my mistakes" : run.today ? "Today's practice" : run.paperName ? run.paperName : run.assignedAt ? "Your set test" : run.mock ? "Mock test" : run.fromMistakes ? topic + ": mistakes practice" : topic;
  $("doneScore").textContent = `${run.marks} / ${run.totalMarks} marks` + (run.totalMarks ? `  (${pctNow}%)` : "");
  const pct = run.totalMarks ? run.marks / run.totalMarks : 0;
  clearInterval(window.__tick);
  const total = run.times.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0), avg = run.times.length ? total / run.times.length : 0;
  let timeMsg = run.times.length ? `Time: ${fmtTime(total)}, about ${Math.round(avg)} seconds a question.` : "";
  if (!run.fromMistakes && answered >= MIN_FOR_BEST) {
    const prev = store.get("avgTime:" + topic);
    if (prev) timeMsg += avg < prev - 1 ? ` That's faster than your last set (${Math.round(prev)} s).` : avg > prev + 1 ? ` Your last set was ${Math.round(prev)} s a question.` : " About the same pace as last time.";
    store.set("avgTime:" + topic, avg);
  }
  $("doneTime").textContent = `${run.score} of ${answered} questions right. ` + timeMsg;
  $("doneMsg").textContent = pct === 1 ? "Perfect score!" : pct >= .8 ? "Great work. Have a look at the ones you missed." : pct >= .5 ? "Good effort. Practising your mistakes will help most." : "Keep going. Read each worked solution, then practise your mistakes.";
  if (run.fixFor) $("doneMsg").textContent = fixedPapers().includes(run.fixFor) ? "Well done. The next paper is now open." : "Answer at least most of the questions to open the next paper.";
  $("retryMissed").hidden = run.missed.length === 0;
  const box = $("missed"); box.innerHTML = run.missed.length ? "<h3>Questions to look at again</h3>" : "";
  if (run.mock) {   /* papers and tests: every question, with the child's answer, the right answer and how to work it out */
    const g = run.given || {}, all = run.items.map(x => x.q).slice().sort((a, b) => String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id), undefined, { numeric: true }));
    const right = all.filter(q => g[q.id] && g[q.id].ok).length, wrong = all.filter(q => g[q.id] && !g[q.id].ok).length, none = all.length - right - wrong;
    box.innerHTML = `<h3>Paper review</h3><p class="muted small">${right} right · ${wrong} wrong · ${none} not answered. Read the working for each one you missed.</p>`;
    all.forEach((q, i) => {
      const r = g[q.id], st = r ? (r.ok ? "ok" : "no") : "none", a = document.createElement("article");
      a.className = "rv " + st;
      a.innerHTML = `<div class="rvhead"><span class="rvmark">${st === "ok" ? "✓" : st === "no" ? "✗" : "–"}</span><b>Question ${stEsc(q.pq || String(i + 1))}</b></div>
        <div class="rvq">${(() => { const [f0, ...r0] = q.q.split(SPLIT)[0].split("\n"), tb = splitTable(r0);
          return tb ? fracHTML(f0) + tableHTML(tb.rows) + tb.other.map(l => " " + fracHTML(l)).join("") : fracHTML(q.q.split(SPLIT)[0].replace(/\n/g, " ").replace(/\s{3,}/g, "  ·  ")); })()}</div>
        <div>Your answer: <b>${r ? fracHTML(stEsc(r.a)) : "not answered"}</b></div>
        ${st === "ok" ? "" : `<div>Correct answer: <b>${fracHTML(q.a)}</b></div>`}
        ${q.s ? `<details${st === "ok" ? "" : " open"}><summary>How to work it out</summary><div class="muted">${fracHTML(q.s)}</div></details>` : ""}`;
      box.appendChild(a);
    });
    run.missed = all.filter(q => !(g[q.id] && g[q.id].ok));
    $("retryMissed").hidden = run.missed.length === 0;
    if (run.paperName) {
      const nm = run.paperName.replace(/ · retake$/, ""), f = firstTry(nm), passed = f && f.marks / f.total >= 0.55;
      $("retryMissed").textContent = "Fix my mistakes (new questions)";
      box.insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", `<p class="planline">${run.retake ? "This was a retake with new questions, so it doesn't change your first-try score." : passed ? "55% or more: the next paper is open." : fixedPapers().includes(nm) ? "The next paper is open." : "To open the next paper, read the working below, then do <b>Fix my mistakes</b>: new questions like the ones you missed."}</p>`);
    } else $("retryMissed").textContent = "Practise my mistakes";
  } else run.missed.forEach(q => {
    const a = document.createElement("article");
    a.innerHTML = `<strong></strong><span></span><span class="muted"></span>`;
    a.children[0].innerHTML = fracHTML(q.q.split(SPLIT)[0].replace(/\n/, " ").replace(/\s{3,}/g, "  ·  "));
    a.children[1].innerHTML = fracHTML("Answer: " + q.a); a.children[2].innerHTML = fracHTML(q.s);
    box.appendChild(a);
  });
  $("cumap").hidden = true; $("again").hidden = !!run.checkup; $("again").textContent = "New questions"; $("again").onclick = againDefault;
  if (run.checkup) finishCheckup();
  renderTopics(); show("done");
}

function readCount() {
  let n = parseInt($("count").value, 10);
  if (!(n >= 1)) n = 10;
  n = Math.min(n, 50);
  $("count").value = n; store.set("count", n);
  return n;
}
const savedCount = store.get("count"); if (savedCount) $("count").value = savedCount;
$("start").onclick = () => { FROM_SETUP = true; try { start(); } finally { FROM_SETUP = false; } };
$("count").onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") $("start").onclick(); };
$("skip").onclick = () => { if (run.i >= run.items.length - 1) return; const it = run.items.splice(run.i, 1)[0]; it.skipped = (it.skipped || 0) + 1; run.items.push(it); renderQ(); };
$("next").onclick = () => { if (Date.now() - (run.answeredAt || 0) < 400) return; run.i++; renderQ(); };
$("quit").onclick = finish;
$("retryMissed").onclick = () => run.paperName && run.mock ? startFix(run.paperName.replace(/ · retake$/, ""), run.missed.slice()) : startMistakes(run.missed.slice());
const againDefault = () => (run && run.checkup ? show("home") : run && run.mock && !run.assignedAt ? startMock() : run && run.assignedAt ? show("home") : run && run.sks ? $("start").onclick() : start());
$("again").onclick = againDefault;
$("home2").onclick = () => show("home");
$("backDone").onclick = () => show("home");
$("backQuiz").onclick = () => (run && run.answered > 0 ? askLeave() : leaveSet());
$("leaveYes").onclick = leaveSet;
$("leaveNo").onclick = () => { $("leave").hidden = true; };
window.addEventListener("popstate", () => {
  if (!$("quiz").hidden && run && run.answered > 0) { try { history.pushState(null, "", "#practice"); } catch (e) {} askLeave(); return; }
  if (!$("quiz").hidden || !$("done").hidden) { clearInterval(window.__tick); show("home", true); }
  if (!$("parents").hidden || !$("printView").hidden || !$("pastView").hidden || !$("raceView").hidden || !$("helpView").hidden || !$("profileView").hidden) { clearInterval(window.__race); show("home", true); }
});
/* ---------- saved progress, edits and parent tools ---------- */
const BASE = QUESTIONS.map(q => ({ ...q }));
const EDITS = new Map();
const TAB_NAMES = ["Number", "Add & Subtract", "Multiply & Divide", "Fractions", "Decimals", "Money & Time"];
let DB = null, UID = null, saveChain = Promise.resolve();
function applyEdits() {
  const out = [];
  BASE.forEach(q => { const e = EDITS.get(q.id); if (!e) out.push(q); else if (!e.del) out.push({ ...q, ...pickQ(e) }); });
  EDITS.forEach((e, id) => { if (!e.del && !BASE.some(q => q.id === id)) out.push({ id, wp: false, ...pickQ(e) }); });
  QUESTIONS.splice(0, QUESTIONS.length, ...out);
}
function pickQ(e) { const o = {}; ["q", "a", "s", "lv", "m", "t", "k", "tab", "pic", "yr"].forEach(k => { if (e[k] != null && e[k] !== "") o[k] = e[k]; }); if (o.m) o.m = +o.m; return o; }

function getHistory() { return store.get("history") || []; }
function progressDoc() { const best = {}; TOPICS.forEach(t => { const b = store.get("bestPct:" + t); if (b != null) best[t] = b; }); return { history: getHistory().slice(-300), best, starter: stAttempts().slice(-24) }; }
function saveProgress() {
  if (!DB || !UID) return;
  const body = progressDoc();
  saveChain = saveChain.then(() => DB.doc("data/users/" + UID + "/progress" + (CHILD ? "-" + CHILD : "")).set(body)).catch(() => {});
}
function mergeProgress(d) {
  const seen = new Set(), all = [];
  [...(d.history || []), ...getHistory()].sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at).forEach(h => { if (!seen.has(h.at)) { seen.add(h.at); all.push(h); } });
  store.set("history", all.slice(-300));
  Object.entries(d.best || {}).forEach(([t, v]) => { const b = store.get("bestPct:" + t); if (b == null || v > b) store.set("bestPct:" + t, v); });
  if (d.starter && d.starter.length) { const seen2 = new Set(), all2 = []; [...d.starter, ...stAttempts()].sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at).forEach(a => { if (!seen2.has(a.at)) { seen2.add(a.at); all2.push(a); } }); store.set("starter:attempts", all2.slice(-24)); }
}
(async () => {
  try {
    if (typeof claude === "undefined" || !claude.use) return;
    const db = await claude.use("db"); if (!db) return;
    DB = db;
    const U = await claude.use("user");
    UID = U ? await U.id() : null;
    DB.collection("edits").onSnapshot(snap => {
      EDITS.clear(); snap.docs.forEach(d => { if (d.exists) EDITS.set(d.id, d.data()); });
      applyEdits();
      if (!$("parents").hidden && !$("pEdit").hidden && $("eForm").hidden) renderEditList();
    }, () => {});
    if (UID) {
      const d = await DB.doc("data/users/" + UID + "/progress" + (CHILD ? "-" + CHILD : "")).get();
      if (d.exists) mergeProgress(d.data()); else if (getHistory().length) saveProgress();
      renderTopics(); renderStreak();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
})();

loadLocalEdits(); applyEdits();
/* streak and stars */
const dayKey = t => { const d = new Date(t); return d.getFullYear() + "-" + (d.getMonth() + 1) + "-" + d.getDate(); };
function streakDays() {
  const days = new Set(getHistory().filter(h => h.answered > 0).map(h => dayKey(h.at)));
  let n = 0, t = Date.now();
  if (!days.has(dayKey(t))) t -= 864e5;
  while (days.has(dayKey(t))) { n++; t -= 864e5; }
  return { n, today: days.has(dayKey(Date.now())) };
}
function renderStreak() {
  const { n, today } = streakDays();
  $("streak").textContent = n ? `${n} day${n > 1 ? "s" : ""} in a row` + (today ? "" : " – practise today to keep it going!") : "";
}
const starsFor = pct => pct == null ? 0 : pct >= 90 ? 3 : pct >= 70 ? 2 : pct >= 50 ? 1 : 0;

function logHistory() {
  const types = {};
  (run.log || []).forEach(l => { const k = l.tab + " · " + l.t; types[k] = types[k] || [0, 0]; types[k][1]++; if (l.ok) types[k][0]++; });
  const h = getHistory();
  if (run.assignedAt) { store.set("assignedDone", run.assignedAt); if (DB) DB.doc("assigned/current").update({ doneAt: Date.now(), marks: run.marks, total: run.totalMarks }).catch(() => {}); }
  h.push({ retake: run.retake || undefined, at: Date.now(), topic: run.assignedAt ? "Set test" : run.paperName ? run.paperName + (run.retake ? " · retake" : "") : run.mock ? "Mock test" : run.label || topic, level: run.mock ? "All" : level, mock: !!run.mock, marks: run.marks, total: run.totalMarks, right: run.score, answered: run.answered, secs: Math.round(run.times.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0)), types });
  store.set("history", h.slice(-300));
  saveProgress(); renderStreak();
}

/* mock test and printable test */
const MOCK_N = 20, MOCK_MIN = 25;
function mixedSet(n, lv) {
  const tops = TOPICS.filter(t => t !== MIXED), per = Math.ceil(n / tops.length), seen = new Set(store.get("seen") || []), list = [];
  const keepLevel = level; level = lv || "All";
  tops.forEach(t => {
    const bank = shuffle(topicPool(t, "All").filter(q => !seen.has(q.id) && !isPicture(q))).slice(0, per - 1);
    list.push(...bank, ...makeGenerated(per - bank.length, t, "All", list.map(q => q.q)));
  });
  level = keepLevel;
  return shuffle(list).slice(0, n);
}
function startMock(set) {
  newRun(false); run.mock = true; run.deadline = set && !set.minutes ? 0 : Date.now() + ((set && set.minutes) || MOCK_MIN) * 60000;
  if (set) run.assignedAt = set.at;
  clearInterval(window.__tick);
  const tick = () => { if (!run || !run.mock) return; if (!run.deadline) { $("qtime").textContent = fmtTime((Date.now() - run.startedAt) / 1000); return; } const left = Math.max(0, (run.deadline - Date.now()) / 1000); $("qtime").textContent = fmtTime(left) + " left"; if (left <= 0) { clearInterval(window.__tick); finish(); } };
  window.__tick = setInterval(tick, 1000); tick();
  addItems(set ? set.items.map(q => ({ ...q })) : mixedSet(MOCK_N));
  show("quiz"); renderQ();
}
function paperHTML(list) {
  const esc = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
  let html = `<h2>Maths practice test</h2><p class="muted">Name: ________________ &nbsp; Date: __________ &nbsp; Time: ${MOCK_MIN} minutes</p>`;
  list.forEach((q, i) => {
    const parts = q.q.split(SPLIT), [first, ...rest] = parts[0].split("\n");
    let t = first; const calc = t.match(/^([^\n]*?[:.])\s+((?:[−\d(IVXLC]|___)[^:\n]*(?:=|___)[^:\n]*)$/); if (calc) t = calc[1] + "\n" + calc[2];
    html += `<div class="pq"><b>${i + 1}.</b> <span class="muted">(${q.m || 1} mark${(q.m || 1) > 1 ? "s" : ""})</span><div>${fracHTML(t).replace(/\n/g, "<br>")}</div>`;
    const ptb = splitTable(rest); if (ptb) { html += tableHTML(ptb.rows) + ptb.other.map(l => `<div>${fracHTML(l)}</div>`).join(""); rest.length = 0; }
    if (rest.length) html += `<div class="seq">${rest.join("   ").split(/\s{3,}/).filter(Boolean).map(x => `<span class="${x === "___" ? "blank" : ""}">${fracHTML(x)}</span>`).join("")}</div>`;
    if (q.pic) html += `<div class="picbox">${drawPic(q.pic)}</div>`;
    if (parts.length > 2) html += `<div class="seq">${parts.slice(1).map(o => `<span>${fracHTML(o)}</span>`).join("")}</div>`;
    html += `<div class="ans">Answer: <span></span></div></div>`;
  });
  html += `<div class="pkey"><h2>Answers</h2><ol>${list.map(q => `<li>${fracHTML(q.a)} <span class="muted">– ${fracHTML(q.s)}</span></li>`).join("")}</ol></div>`;
  return html;
}
const getPicks = () => store.get("printPicks") || [];
function makePrint() {
  const n = Math.max(5, Math.min(40, parseInt($("pCount").value, 10) || 20)); $("pCount").value = n;
  const lv = $("pLevel").value;
  let list = [];
  if ($("pPicked").checked) list = getPicks().map(id => QUESTIONS.find(q => q.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
  if (list.length < n && !$("pPicked").checked) list = mixedSet(n, lv);
  window.__printList = list;
  $("paper").innerHTML = list.length ? paperHTML(list) : '<p class="muted">No questions picked yet.</p>';
}
function renderCurTest() {
  const a = ASSIGNED, el = $("curTest");
  if (!a || !a.items || !a.items.length) { el.innerHTML = ""; return; }
  el.innerHTML = `<span class="muted">A test (${a.items.length} questions) is already set. Setting a new one replaces it.</span> <button type="button" class="ghost danger" id="removeTest2">Remove it</button>`;
  $("removeTest2").onclick = async () => { ASSIGNED = null; store.set("assigned", null); renderAssigned(); if (DB) { try { await DB.doc("assigned/current").delete(); } catch (e) {} } el.innerHTML = '<span class="muted">Test removed.</span>'; };
}
function showPrint() { renderCurTest(); $("pPickN").textContent = getPicks().length; $("pPicked").checked = getPicks().length > 0; makePrint(); showPanel("printView"); }

/* parents area */
function showPanel(id) {
  setView(id);
  ["home", "quiz", "done", "parents", "printView", "pastView", "raceView", "helpView", "starterView", "profileView"].forEach(s => $(s).hidden = s !== id);
  $("homebar").hidden = id !== "home"; $("todayBtn").hidden = id !== "home"; $("starterBtn").hidden = id !== "home"; if (id !== "home") { $("planLine").hidden = true; $("plan2Btn").hidden = true; } renderAssigned(); if (id === "home") { renderSuggest(); renderToday(); applySettings(); }
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  try { history.pushState(null, "", "#" + id); } catch (e) {}
}
function openParents() {
  const pin = store.get("parentPin");
  $("pinMsg").textContent = pin ? "Enter the parent PIN." : "Choose a 4-digit parent PIN. It keeps children out of this area on this device.";
  $("pinBox").hidden = false; $("parentArea").hidden = true; $("pin").value = "";
  showPanel("parents"); $("pin").focus();
}
function checkPin() {
  const v = $("pin").value.trim(), pin = store.get("parentPin");
  if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(v)) { $("pinMsg").textContent = "The PIN is 4 digits."; return; }
  if (!pin) store.set("parentPin", v); else if (v !== pin) { $("pinMsg").textContent = "That PIN isn't right."; $("pin").value = ""; return; }
  $("pinBox").hidden = true; $("parentArea").hidden = false; parentTab("kids");
}
function parentTab(t) {
  [...$("ptabs").children].forEach(b => b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", b.dataset.tab === t));
  $("pKids").hidden = t !== "kids"; $("pProgress").hidden = t !== "progress"; $("pEdit").hidden = t !== "edit"; $("pSet").hidden = t !== "setTest"; $("pSettings").hidden = t !== "settings"; $("pReports").hidden = t !== "reports"; $("pPlan").hidden = t !== "plan";
  if (t === "kids") renderKids(); else if (t === "progress") renderProgress(); else if (t === "edit") renderEditor(); else if (t === "settings") renderSettings(); else if (t === "reports") renderReports(); else if (t === "plan") renderPlan(); else renderSetTest();
}
function renderProgress() {
  const h = getHistory().filter(x => x.answered > 0), box = $("pProgress");
  if (!h.length) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="muted">No practice yet. Results show here after the first set.</p>'; return; }
  const week = h.filter(x => x.at > Date.now() - 7 * 864e5);
  const pct = xs => { const m = xs.reduce((a, x) => a + x.marks, 0), t = xs.reduce((a, x) => a + x.total, 0); return t ? Math.round(m / t * 100) : 0; };
  const qs = xs => xs.reduce((a, x) => a + x.answered, 0);
  const types = {};
  h.forEach(x => Object.entries(x.types || {}).forEach(([k, [r, n]]) => { types[k] = types[k] || [0, 0]; types[k][0] += r; types[k][1] += n; }));
  const weak = Object.entries(types).filter(([, [, n]]) => n >= 3).map(([k, [r, n]]) => [k, Math.round(r / n * 100), n]).sort((a, b) => a[1] - b[1]).slice(0, 6);
  const byTopic = {};
  h.filter(x => !x.mock).forEach(x => { (byTopic[x.topic] = byTopic[x.topic] || []).push(x); });
  const esc = s => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;");
  const d = t => new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
  box.innerHTML = `<div class="kpis">
      <div class="kpi"><b>${streakDays().n}</b>days in a row</div>
      <div class="kpi"><b>${qs(week)}</b>questions this week</div>
      <div class="kpi"><b>${pct(week)}%</b>marks this week</div>
      <div class="kpi"><b>${h.length}</b>sets done</div></div>
    <h3>By topic</h3><table class="ptable">${Object.entries(byTopic).map(([t, xs]) => `<tr><td>${esc(t)}</td><td><div class="pbar"><i style="width:${pct(xs)}%"></i></div></td><td class="n">${pct(xs)}%</td><td class="n">${qs(xs)} q</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>
    <h3>Needs practice</h3>${weak.length ? `<table class="ptable">${weak.map(([k, p, n]) => `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td class="n">${p}% right</td><td class="n">${n} tried</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>` : '<p class="muted">Shows once a kind of question has been tried 3 times.</p>'}
    <h3>Recent sets</h3><table class="ptable"><tr><th>Date</th><th>Topic</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th class="n">Time</th></tr>${h.slice(-12).reverse().map(x => `<tr><td>${d(x.at)}</td><td>${esc(x.topic)}${x.level && x.level !== "All" ? " (" + x.level + ")" : ""}</td><td class="n">${x.marks}/${x.total}</td><td class="n">${fmtTime(x.secs || 0)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>
    <p class="muted small">${DB && UID ? "Saved to your Claude account, so it shows on any device." : "Saved on this device."}</p>${starterParentHTML()}`;
  stWireMap(box);
}
let editing = null;
function renderEditor() {
  $("editNote").textContent = `Year ${YEAR} questions (change the year at the top). ` + (DB ? "Changes save to your Claude account and show on every device at once." : "Changes save on this device. Sign in to Claude to share them across devices.");
  if (!$("eTab").options.length) { TAB_NAMES.forEach(t => { const o = document.createElement("option"); o.textContent = t; $("eTab").appendChild(o); }); }
  $("eForm").hidden = true; renderEditList();
}
function renderEditList() {
  const tab = $("eTab").value, lv = $("eLevel").value, s = $("eSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = QUESTIONS.filter(q => q.tab === tab && (q.yr || 4) === YEAR && (lv === "All" || q.lv === lv) && (!s || (q.q + " " + q.a + " " + q.id + " " + q.t).toLowerCase().includes(s)));
  const box = $("eList"); box.innerHTML = `<p class="muted small">${list.length} questions · <span id="pickCount">${getPicks().length}</span> picked for a test</p><div class="row pickrow"><button type="button" class="primary" id="usePicks">Make test from picks</button><button type="button" class="ghost" id="clearPicks">Clear picks</button></div>`;
  box.querySelector("#usePicks").onclick = () => { if (getPicks().length) showPrint(); };
  box.querySelector("#clearPicks").onclick = () => { store.set("printPicks", []); renderEditList(); };
  list.forEach(q => {
    const b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button";
    b.innerHTML = `<span></span><span class="meta"></span>`;
    b.children[0].textContent = q.q.split(SPLIT)[0].replace(/\n/g, "  ").slice(0, 140);
    b.children[1].textContent = `${q.lv || ""} · ${q.m || 1} mark${(q.m || 1) > 1 ? "s" : ""} · ${q.t} · answer ${q.a}`;
    if (EDITS.has(q.id)) b.children[1].insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", '<span class="badge">changed</span>');
    b.onclick = () => openForm(q);
    const row = document.createElement("div"); row.className = "erow";
    const lab = document.createElement("label"); lab.className = "pick";
    const cb = document.createElement("input"); cb.type = "checkbox"; cb.checked = getPicks().includes(q.id); cb.setAttribute("aria-label", "Pick for a test"); lab.title = "Pick for a test";
    cb.onchange = () => { const p = getPicks().filter(x => x !== q.id); if (cb.checked) p.push(q.id); store.set("printPicks", p); $("pickCount").textContent = p.length; };
    lab.append(cb); row.append(b, lab); box.appendChild(row);
  });
}
function nextId(tab) {
  const ids = QUESTIONS.concat(BASE).filter(q => q.tab === tab).map(q => q.id), pre = (ids[0] || "Q-Y4-000").replace(/-\d+[a-z]?$/, "");
  const max = Math.max(0, ...ids.map(i => parseInt((i.match(/-(\d+)[a-z]?$/) || [0, 0])[1], 10)));
  return pre + "-" + String(max + 1).padStart(3, "0");
}
function openForm(q) {
  const tab = $("eTab").value;
  editing = q ? q.id : nextId(tab);
  const v = q || { q: "", a: "", s: "", lv: "Easy", m: 1, t: "", pic: "" };
  $("eFormTitle").textContent = q ? "Edit " + q.id : "New question " + editing;
  $("fQ").value = v.q; $("fA").value = v.a; $("fS").value = v.s; $("fLv").value = v.lv || "Easy"; $("fM").value = v.m || 1; $("fT").value = v.t || ""; $("fPic").value = v.pic || "";
  $("typeList").innerHTML = [...new Set(QUESTIONS.filter(x => x.tab === tab).map(x => x.t))].map(t => `<option value="${t.replace(/"/g, "&quot;")}">`).join("");
  $("fReset").hidden = !(q && EDITS.has(q.id) && BASE.some(b => b.id === q.id)); $("fDel").hidden = !q; $("fMsg").textContent = "";
  $("eForm").hidden = false; previewForm(); $("eForm").scrollIntoView({ block: "start" });
}
function previewForm() { const p = $("fPic").value.trim(); $("fPreview").innerHTML = ""; const t = document.createElement("p"); t.className = "question"; t.textContent = $("fQ").value.split(SPLIT)[0]; $("fPreview").appendChild(t); if (p) $("fPreview").insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", drawPic(p)); }
function localEdit(id, body) {
  const all = store.get("localEdits") || {};
  if (body) all[id] = body; else delete all[id];
  store.set("localEdits", all); loadLocalEdits(); applyEdits();
}
function loadLocalEdits() { if (DB) return; EDITS.clear(); Object.entries(store.get("localEdits") || {}).forEach(([id, e]) => EDITS.set(id, e)); }
async function saveForm(body) {
  if (!DB) { localEdit(editing, { ...body, at: Date.now() }); $("fMsg").textContent = "Saved on this device."; $("eForm").hidden = true; renderEditList(); return; }
  $("fMsg").textContent = "Saving…";
  try { await DB.collection("edits").doc(editing).set({ ...body, at: Date.now() }); $("fMsg").textContent = "Saved."; $("eForm").hidden = true; renderEditList(); }
  catch (e) { $("fMsg").textContent = "Couldn't save (" + (e && e.code || "error") + ")."; }
}

};

{ const o = store.get("bestPct:Add, subtract, multiply, divide") ?? store.get("bestPct:Four operations"); if (o != null) ["Add and subtract", "Multiply and divide"].forEach(t => { if (store.get("bestPct:" + t) == null) store.set("bestPct:" + t, o); }); }

/* tests set by a parent */
let ASSIGNED = store.get("assigned");

  const a = ASSIGNED, show = a && a.items && a.items.length && store.get("assignedDone") !== a.at && !a.doneAt;
  $("assigned").hidden = !show || $("home").hidden;
  if (show) $("assignedInfo").textContent = `${a.items.length} questions` + (a.minutes ? `, ${a.minutes} minutes` : ", no time limit");
}

$("setTest").onclick = async () => {
  const items = (window.__printList || []).map(q => { const o = {}; ["id", "q", "a", "s", "t", "tab", "m", "lv", "pic", "wrong", "fixed", "gen"].forEach(k => { if (q[k] != null) o[k] = q[k]; }); return o; });
  if (!items.length) { $("setMsg").textContent = "Make a test first."; return; }
  const mins = Math.max(0, Math.min(90, parseInt($("pMins").value, 10) || 0));
  ASSIGNED = { at: Date.now(), minutes: mins, items };
  store.set("assigned", ASSIGNED); store.set("assignedDone", null); renderCurTest();
  $("setMsg").textContent = "Set. It shows on your child's home screen.";
  if (DB) { try { await DB.doc("assigned/current").set(ASSIGNED); $("setMsg").textContent = "Set. It shows on the home screen of any device signed in to your Claude account."; } catch (e) {} }
};
(async () => {
  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !DB; i++) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));
  if (!DB) return;
  DB.doc("assigned/current").onSnapshot(d => { if (!d.exists) { if (ASSIGNED && !ASSIGNED.local) { ASSIGNED = null; store.set("assigned", null); renderAssigned(); if (!$("pSet").hidden) renderSetTest(); } return; } if (d.exists) { const a = d.data(); if (!ASSIGNED || a.at >= ASSIGNED.at) { ASSIGNED = { ...a }; store.set("assigned", ASSIGNED); renderAssigned(); } } }, () => {});
})();

function renderSetTest() {
  const a = ASSIGNED, box = $("pSet");
  const mk = '<div class="row"><button class="primary" id="makeTestBtn" type="button">Make a test</button></div>';
  if (!a || !a.items || !a.items.length) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="muted">No test is set at the moment.</p>' + mk; $("makeTestBtn").onclick = showPrint; return; }
  const done = a.doneAt || store.get("assignedDone") === a.at;
  const when = new Date(a.at).toLocaleString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
  box.innerHTML = `<h3>Test set for your child</h3>
    <div class="row"><button class="primary danger-fill" id="removeTest" type="button">Remove this test</button><button class="ghost" id="makeTestBtn" type="button">Make a new test</button></div>
    <div class="kpis"><div class="kpi"><b>${a.items.length}</b>questions</div><div class="kpi"><b>${a.minutes || "–"}</b>${a.minutes ? "minutes" : "no time limit"}</div><div class="kpi"><b>${done ? "Done" : "Waiting"}</b>${done && a.total ? a.marks + " / " + a.total + " marks" : "set " + when}</div></div>
    <h3>Questions</h3>
    <ol class="setlist">${a.items.map(q => `<li>${fracHTML(q.q.split(SPLIT)[0].replace(/\n/g, "  "))}</li>`).join("")}</ol>`;
  $("makeTestBtn").onclick = showPrint;
  $("removeTest").onclick = async () => {
    const b = $("removeTest");
    if (!b.dataset.sure) { b.dataset.sure = 1; b.textContent = "Tap again to remove"; setTimeout(() => { if (b.isConnected) { delete b.dataset.sure; b.textContent = "Remove this test"; } }, 3000); return; }
    ASSIGNED = null; store.set("assigned", null); renderAssigned();
    if (DB) { try { await DB.doc("assigned/current").delete(); } catch (e) {} }
    renderSetTest(); $("pSet").insertAdjacentHTML("afterbegin", '<p class="muted">Test removed.</p>');
  };
}

/* past papers: links and score log */
function renderPast() {
  const h = getHistory().filter(x => x.paper).reverse(), box = $("ppList");
  if (!h.length) { box.innerHTML = '<p class="muted">No scores yet.</p>'; return; }
  const d = t => new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" });
  box.innerHTML = `<table class="ptable"><tr><th>Date</th><th>Paper</th><th class="n">Score</th><th class="n">%</th><th class="n">Time</th></tr>${h.map(x => `<tr><td>${d(x.at)}</td><td>${escH(x.topic)}</td><td class="n">${x.marks}/${x.total}</td><td class="n">${Math.round(x.marks / x.total * 100)}%</td><td class="n">${x.secs ? Math.round(x.secs / 60) + " min" : ""}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>`;
}
const PRACTICE_TESTS = Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => ["PT" + (i + 1), "Practice test " + (i + 1)]);
/* papers open one after another: the next opens after 55% or more on the FIRST try of this one,
   or after the child has done "Fix my mistakes" for it (new questions of the same kinds, so nothing can be copied).
   Retakes use a twin paper (same order, new questions) and never count as the score. */
const firstTry = name => getHistory().find(h => h.topic === name && h.total && !h.retake);
const fixedPapers = () => store.get("fixedPapers") || [];
function paperPassed(name) { const f = firstTry(name); return !!f && (f.marks / f.total >= 0.55 || fixedPapers().includes(name)); }
let SETS_BY_SKILL = null;
function similarSets(sk) {   /* practice sets of one part, each as a list of questions in a-b-c order */
  if (!SETS_BY_SKILL) { SETS_BY_SKILL = {}; const g = {};
    QUESTIONS.forEach(q => { if ((q.yr || 4) !== 5 || q.paper || !q.skill) return; const k = q.set || q.id; (g[k] = g[k] || []).push(q); });
    Object.values(g).forEach(v => { v.sort((a, b) => String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id))); (SETS_BY_SKILL[v[0].skill] = SETS_BY_SKILL[v[0].skill] || []).push(v); }); }
  return SETS_BY_SKILL[sk] || [];
}
function twinPaper(code) {   /* same questions in the same order, each swapped for a fresh one of the same part */
  const seen = new Set(store.get("seen") || []), used = new Set(), groups = {};
  QUESTIONS.filter(q => q.paper === code).sort((a, b) => String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id), undefined, { numeric: true }))
    .forEach(q => { const n = String(q.id).replace(/[a-z]$/, ""); (groups[n] = groups[n] || []).push(q); });
  const out = [];
  Object.values(groups).forEach((grp, gi) => {
    const mixed = grp.some(q => q.skill !== grp[0].skill);   /* a question that mixes parts: match each part on its own */
    const sets = mixed ? [] : shuffle(similarSets(grp[0].skill).filter(v => v.every(q => q.skill === grp[0].skill) && !v.some(q => used.has(q.id))));
    const pick = mixed ? null : sets.find(v => v.length === grp.length && !v.some(q => seen.has(q.id))) || sets.find(v => v.length === grp.length) || sets.find(v => v.length >= grp.length) || null;
    if (pick) pick.forEach(q => used.add(q.id));
    grp.forEach((orig, k) => {
      const q = pick ? pick[k] : shuffle(similarSets(orig.skill).flat()).find(x => !used.has(x.id));
      if (q) { used.add(q.id); out.push({ ...q, pq: (gi + 1) + "abcde"[k], m: orig.m || 1 }); } else out.push({ ...orig });
    });
  });
  return out;
}
function startFix(name, missed) {   /* new questions of the same kinds as the ones missed; finishing it opens the next paper */
  const seen = new Set(store.get("seen") || []), used = new Set(missed.map(q => q.id)), list = [];
  missed.slice(0, 20).forEach(q => {
    const pool = shuffle(similarSets(q.skill).flat().filter(x => !used.has(x.id)));
    const x = pool.find(x => !seen.has(x.id)) || pool[0]; if (x) { used.add(x.id); list.push(x); }
  });
  if (!list.length) return;
  newRun(true); run.fixFor = name; addItems(list); show("quiz"); renderQ();
}
function papersOpenTo() {   /* highest paper number that is open; remembered so it never closes again */
  const list = PRACTICE_TESTS.filter(([c]) => QUESTIONS.some(q => q.paper === c));
  let n = 1; while (n < list.length && paperPassed(list[n - 1][1])) n++;
  n = Math.max(n, store.get("ptOpen") || 1); store.set("ptOpen", n);
  return hasFull() ? n : Math.min(n, FREE.papers);
}
function renderPracticeTests() {
  const box = $("ptList"); box.innerHTML = ""; box.className = "ptgrid";
  const openTo = papersOpenTo();
  let i = 0;
  PRACTICE_TESTS.forEach(([code, name]) => {
    const items = QUESTIONS.filter(q => q.paper === code); if (!items.length) return;
    i++; const locked = i > openTo, prev = "Paper " + (i - 1);
    const b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "ptile" + (locked ? " locked" : "");
    const f = firstTry(name), tot = items.reduce((a, q) => a + (q.m || 1), 0);
    const lockMsg = !hasFull() && i > FREE.papers ? "Full access" : `Opens after 55% on ${prev}, or after fixing its mistakes`;
    b.innerHTML = `<b>${name.replace("Practice test", "Paper")}</b><span class="muted small">${locked ? lockMsg : f ? `First try ${f.marks} / ${tot} · retake has new questions` : "60 marks · 60 min"}</span>`;
    if (f) b.classList.add("done");
    if (locked) { b.disabled = true; b.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true"); }
    else b.onclick = () => { const retake = !!f; startMock({ items: retake ? twinPaper(code) : items, minutes: 60 }); run.paperName = name; run.retake = retake; renderQ(); };
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
}
function openPapers(mode) { $("pvPT").hidden = mode !== "pt"; $("pvPast").hidden = mode !== "past"; renderPast(); renderPracticeTests(); showPanel("pastView"); }
$("pastBtn").onclick = () => openPapers("past");
$("ptBtn").onclick = () => openPapers("pt");
$("backPast").onclick = () => showPanel("home");
$("ppSave").onclick = () => {
  const name = $("ppName").value.trim() || "Past paper", m = parseInt($("ppMarks").value, 10), t = parseInt($("ppTotal").value, 10), mins = parseInt($("ppMins").value, 10) || 0;
  if (isNaN(m) || !t || m < 0 || m > t) { $("ppMsg").textContent = "Enter the marks scored and the total (marks can't be more than the total)."; return; }
  const h = getHistory(); h.push({ at: Date.now(), topic: name, paper: true, level: "All", mock: true, marks: m, total: t, right: 0, answered: 1, secs: mins * 60, types: {} });
  store.set("history", h.slice(-300)); saveProgress(); renderStreak();
  $("ppMsg").textContent = `Saved: ${m}/${t} (${Math.round(m / t * 100)}%).`; $("ppMarks").value = ""; renderPast();
};

/* "Practise next": weakest parts from history */
function subFor(tab, t) {
  for (const tp of Object.keys(TREE)) for (const sub of Object.keys(TREE[tp])) if (TREE[tp][sub].some(x => x.tab === tab && x.types.includes(t))) return [tp, sub];
  return null;
}
function renderSuggest() {
  const tally = {};
  getHistory().slice(-60).forEach(x => Object.entries(x.types || {}).forEach(([k, [r, n]]) => { const [tab, t] = k.split(" · "); const ts = subFor(tab, t); if (!ts) return; const key = ts.join("|"); tally[key] = tally[key] || [0, 0]; tally[key][0] += r; tally[key][1] += n; }));
  let weak = Object.entries(tally).filter(([, [r, n]]) => n >= 3 && r / n < 0.8).sort((a, b) => a[1][0] / a[1][1] - b[1][0] / b[1][1]).slice(0, 3);
  const cu = store.get(cuKey());
  if (cu && weak.length < 3) cu.weakSubs.filter(k => !weak.some(([w]) => w === k) && !(tally[k] && tally[k][1] >= 5 && tally[k][0] / tally[k][1] >= 0.8)).slice(0, 3 - weak.length).forEach(k => weak.push([k, [0, 0]]));
  $("suggest").hidden = !weak.length;
  const box = $("suggestBtns"); box.innerHTML = "";
  weak.forEach(([key]) => {
    const [tp, sub] = key.split("|"), b = document.createElement("button");
    b.type = "button"; b.className = "ghost"; b.textContent = `${tp}: ${sub}`;
    b.onclick = () => { topic = tp; type = sub; level = AUTO; start(); };
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
}


/* hints: show the question another way before the second try */
function pvGrid(n) {
  const d = String(n).padStart(4, " ").slice(-4).split(""), heads = ["Th", "H", "T", "O"];
  return `<table class="pvgrid" aria-label="Place value grid"><tr>${heads.map(h => `<th>${h}</th>`).join("")}</tr><tr>${d.map(x => `<td>${x.trim()}</td>`).join("")}</tr></table>`;
}
function hintFor(q) {
  const text = q.q.split(SPLIT)[0], flat = text.replace(/\n/g, "   ");
  const nums = (flat.match(/−?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g) || []).map(x => +x.replace("−", "-"));
  const say = (pic, msg) => `<div class="hint"><span class="hint-msg">${fracHTML(msg)}</span>${pic ? `<span class="hint-pic">${pic}</span>` : ""}</div>`;
  if (q.hint) return say("", q.hint);
  if (q.pic && /^line/.test(q.pic) && /arrow/i.test(flat)) return say("", "First work out what each jump is worth: (end number − start number) ÷ number of jumps. Then count the jumps to the arrow.");
  if (/___\s{5,}<|<\s+>\s+=/.test(q.q) && !nums.some(x => x < 0) && /[+−×÷-]\s*\d/.test(flat.replace(/<|>|=/g, ""))) return say("", "Work out each side first, then compare the two answers.");
  if (/January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December/.test(flat) && /days/i.test(flat)) return say("", "30 days have September, April, June and November. All the rest have 31, except February: 28 days (29 in a leap year). Find each month, then add them.");
  if (/born|birthday/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Take the earlier year away from the later year to find the age. To find a year, add or take away the age.");
  if (/years? old|older|younger|How old/i.test(flat) && !/halve|doubl/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Draw a bar for each person. Older means add the difference; younger means take it away. Everyone gets older by the same number of years.");
  if (new Set((flat.replace(/school year/gi, "").match(/\b(second|minute|hour|day|week|month|year)s?\b/gi) || []).map(u => u.toLowerCase().replace(/s$/, ""))).size >= 2 && !/clock/i.test(flat)) return say("", "60 seconds = 1 minute, 60 minutes = 1 hour, 24 hours = 1 day, 7 days = 1 week, 12 months = 1 year.");
  if (/=/.test(flat) && /___/.test(flat) && /\d{4} = |= \d{4}$|\d{4}$/.test(flat) && /\+/.test(flat) && !/×/.test(flat)) { const v = nums.find(x => x >= 1000 && x <= 9999 && x % 1000); if (v) return say(pvGrid(v), `Put ${v} in a place value grid. Each digit shows how many thousands, hundreds, tens and ones.`); }
  if (/halve|doubl/i.test(flat) && /answer is|gets? \d|How old|What was (my|his|her)/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Work backwards from the answer and undo each step in reverse order. To undo halving, double. To undo doubling, halve.");
  if (q.t === "Think of a number" || /think of a number|thinks of a number|What was my number|What number did I/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Work backwards: start from the answer and undo each step in reverse order. Undo + with −, × with ÷.");
  if (/\b(even|odd) numbers?\b/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Even numbers end in 0, 2, 4, 6 or 8. Odd numbers end in 1, 3, 5, 7 or 9.");
  if (/what fraction/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Write it as a fraction: the part on top, the whole underneath. Then see if you can simplify it.");
  if (/how long|how many minutes|journey took|did it take/i.test(flat) && (/\d:\d\d/.test(flat) || (q.pic && /^clock/.test(q.pic)))) return say("", "Read the start time first. Count on to the next o'clock, then on to the end time. Add the jumps together.");
  if (/24-hour/i.test(flat) || (/\d:\d\d/.test(flat) && /\b(1[3-9]|2[0-3]):\d\d\b/.test(flat))) return say("", "In the afternoon and evening, add 12 to the hours: 3:45 pm is 15:45.");
  { const im = flat.match(/(\d+)\/(\d+)/); if (im && +im[1] > +im[2] && /mixed/i.test(flat)) return say("", `${im[2]}/${im[2]} makes 1 whole. How many wholes fit into ${im[1]}/${im[2]}, and what is left over?`); }
  const rn = flat.match(/nearest (10|100|1000)\b/);
  if (rn && /round/i.test(flat)) {
    const n = +rn[1], v = nums.find(x => x !== n && x > n);
    if (v != null) { const lo = Math.floor(v / n) * n, hi = lo + n, step = n / 10; return say(picLine(lo, hi, 10, (v - lo) / step, [lo, "", "", "", "", lo + n / 2, "", "", "", "", hi]), `${v} is between ${lo} and ${hi}. Which one is it nearer? Halfway is ${lo + n / 2}.`); }
  }
  if (nums.some(x => x < 0) || /°C|below zero|negative/i.test(flat)) {
    const lo = Math.min(...nums, 0), hi = Math.max(...nums, 0), a = Math.floor(lo / 2) * 2 - 2, b = Math.ceil(hi / 2) * 2 + 2, span = b - a;
    if (span <= 40) { const st = span <= 20 ? 1 : 2, steps = span / st, labels = []; for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) { const v = a + i * st; labels.push(v % (span <= 20 ? 2 : 4) === 0 ? v : ""); } return say(picLine(a, b, steps, null, labels), "Use the number line. Left is smaller, right is bigger. Count the jumps across 0."); }
  }
  if (q.tab === "Fractions" || q.tab === "Decimals") {
    if (/^(Adding|Subtracting), same denominator$/.test(q.t)) return say("", "The denominators are the same, so add or take away the top numbers (numerators). The bottom number stays the same.");
    if (/halves|thirds|quarters|fifths|sixths|eighths|tenths/.test(flat) && /how many|in quarters/i.test(flat)) return say("", "Each whole has as many parts as the bottom number. Count the parts in the wholes, then add the extra parts.");
    if (q.t === "Fraction of an amount" || (q.t === "Word problems" && /\d+\/\d+ of/.test(flat))) return say("", "Find one part first: divide by the bottom number. Then multiply by the top number.");
    if (q.t === "Missing number in equivalent fractions") return say("", "Whatever you multiply the bottom number by, multiply the top number by the same.");
    if (q.t === "Comparing fractions" || q.t === "Ordering fractions") return say("", "Same bottom numbers: compare the top numbers. Same top numbers: the smaller bottom number gives the bigger fraction.");
    if (q.t === "Fraction sequences") return say("", "Look at how the top number changes each time. The bottom number stays the same.");
    if (q.t === "Simplest form") return say("", "Find a number that divides into both the top and the bottom. Divide both by it, and keep going until you can't.");
  }
  const fr = flat.match(/(\d+)\/(\d+)/);
  if (fr && +fr[2] <= 12 && +fr[1] <= +fr[2]) return say(picBar(+fr[1], +fr[2]), `${fr[1]}/${fr[2]} means ${fr[1]} out of ${fr[2]} equal parts.`);
  if (q.t === "Short division") return say("", "Start on the left. Divide one digit at a time, and carry anything left over to the next digit.");
  if (q.t === "Column multiplication" && !/□|___/.test(flat)) return say("", "Multiply the ones first, then the tens, then the hundreds. Carry when an answer is 10 or more.");
  const mul = flat.match(/(\d+) × (\d+)/);
  if (mul && +mul[1] <= 12 && +mul[2] <= 12) { const a = +mul[1], b = +mul[2], from = Math.max(1, b - 3), rows = []; for (let k = from; k < b; k++) rows.push(`${k}=${a * k}`); rows.push(`${b}=?`); return say(drawPic(`times ${a} ${rows.join(" ")}`), `Count on in ${a}s from a fact you know.`); }
  const dv = flat.match(/(\d+) ÷ (\d+)/);
  if (dv && +dv[2] <= 12 && +dv[1] <= 144 && +dv[1] >= +dv[2]) return say("", `Think: which number times ${dv[2]} makes ${dv[1]}?`);
  const add = flat.match(/(\d{2,4}) \+ (\d{2,4})(?: \+ (\d{2,4}))?/);
  if (add && !/□|___/.test(flat)) return say(drawPic(`column ${[add[1], add[2], add[3]].filter(Boolean).join("|")}|`), "Set it out in columns. Start with the ones and carry when a column makes 10 or more.");
  if (/[IVXLC]{2,}|Roman/.test(flat)) return say("", "I = 1, V = 5, X = 10, L = 50, C = 100. A smaller letter before a bigger one means take it away.");
  if (q.pic && /^clock/.test(q.pic)) return say("", "The short hand shows the hour. Each number is 5 minutes for the long hand.");
  if (/which place|value of the|more than|less than|missing number|largest|smallest/i.test(flat)) { const v = nums.find(x => x >= 1000 && x <= 9999 && x !== 1000); if (v) return say(pvGrid(v), `Put ${v} in a place value grid: thousands, hundreds, tens, ones.`); }
  const lines = text.split("\n");
  if (lines.length > 1 && /sequence|next|missing|counting|count|steps|___/i.test(text)) { const seq = lines[1].split(/\s{3,}/).map(x => x.replace("−", "-")); for (let i = 0; i + 1 < seq.length; i++) if (!isNaN(+seq[i]) && !isNaN(+seq[i + 1]) && seq[i] !== "" && seq[i + 1] !== "") { const d = +seq[i + 1] - +seq[i]; return say("", `Look at the jump from ${seq[i].replace("-", "−")} to ${seq[i + 1].replace("-", "−")}: it is ${d > 0 ? "+" : "−"}${Math.abs(d)}. Does it stay the same?`); } }
  const h = helpFor(q); if (h) return say("", h.intro);
  return "";
}

/* help cards */
function subOf(q) { for (const tp of Object.keys(TREE)) for (const sub of Object.keys(TREE[tp])) if (TREE[tp][sub].some(x => x.tab === q.tab && (x.wp ? !!q.wp : x.types.includes(q.t)))) return tp + "|" + sub; return null; }
const HELP5 = HELP.__y5 || {};
function helpFor(q) { if (q.skill && HELP5[q.skill]) return HELP5[q.skill]; const k = subOf(q); return (k && HELP[k]) || null; }
function helpBack(h) {
  return `<span class="hintro">${fracHTML(h.intro)}</span>${h.pic ? `<span class="hpic">${drawPic(h.pic)}</span>` : ""}<span class="hex">${h.examples.map(([q, a, p]) => `<span class="hq">${fracHTML(q)}</span><span class="ha">${fracHTML(a)}</span>${p ? `<span class="hpic">${drawPic(p)}</span>` : ""}`).join("")}</span>`;
}
function openHelp(tp, sub, back) {
  if (YEAR === 5) {   /* one card per part of the picked topics (or every part) */
    const sks = PARTS.size ? [...PARTS].map(p => SKILL_BY_NAME[p]) : PICKS.size ? [...PICKS].flatMap(t => MAIN[t] || []) : Object.values(MAIN).flat();
    $("helpTitle").textContent = "Help cards: " + (PICKS.size === 1 && !PARTS.size ? [...PICKS][0] : PICKS.size ? "your topics" : "all topics");
    const box = $("helpCards"); box.innerHTML = "";
    sks.filter(k => HELP5[k]).forEach(k => {
      const h = HELP5[k], tp5 = Object.keys(MAIN).find(t => MAIN[t].includes(k)) || "", b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "card-flip";
      b.innerHTML = `<span class="sub">${escH(tp5)}</span><span class="front">${escH(SKILL_NAME[k] || k)}</span><span class="peek">Tap to see how it works</span><span class="back">${helpBack(h)}</span>`;
      b.onclick = () => b.classList.toggle("open"); box.appendChild(b);
    });
    if (box.children.length === 1) box.firstChild.classList.add("open");
    window.__helpBack = back; showPanel("helpView"); return;
  }
  const live = k => { const [t, sb] = k.split("|"); return !TREE[t] || !TREE[t][sb] || withLevel("All", () => topicPool(t, sb).length) || generatorsFor(t, sb).length; };
  const keys = Object.keys(HELP).filter(k => (tp === MIXED || (k.startsWith(tp + "|") && (sub === "All" || k === tp + "|" + sub))) && live(k));
  $("helpTitle").textContent = "Help cards: " + (tp === MIXED ? "all topics" : sub === "All" ? tp : sub);
  const box = $("helpCards"); box.innerHTML = "";
  keys.forEach(k => {
    const h = HELP[k], b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "card-flip";
    b.innerHTML = `<span class="sub">${escH(k.split("|")[0])}</span><span class="front">${escH(k.split("|")[1])}</span><span class="peek">Tap to see how it works</span><span class="back">${helpBack(h)}</span>`;
    b.onclick = () => b.classList.toggle("open");
    box.appendChild(b);
  });
  if (keys.length === 1) box.firstChild.classList.add("open");
  window.__helpBack = back; showPanel("helpView");
}
$("helpBtn").onclick = () => openHelp(topic, type, "home");
$("backHelp").onclick = () => showPanel(window.__helpBack || "home");

/* mistakes come back: after 1, 3 and 7 days, until right twice */
const DAY = 864e5, GAPS = [1, 3, 7];
const reviewKey = q => q.id && q.id !== "Generated" ? q.id : "g:" + q.q;
function reviewMark(q, earned) {
  const R = store.get("review") || {}, k = reviewKey(q);
  if (!earned) { const c = {}; ["id", "q", "a", "s", "t", "tab", "m", "lv", "pic", "wrong", "fixed", "gen", "k", "wp"].forEach(f => { if (q[f] != null) c[f] = q[f]; }); R[k] = { q: c, due: Date.now() + DAY, box: 0 }; }
  else if (R[k] && R[k].due <= Date.now() + 36e5) { R[k].box++; if (R[k].box >= 2) delete R[k]; else R[k].due = Date.now() + GAPS[R[k].box] * DAY; }
  const keys = Object.keys(R); if (keys.length > 200) keys.sort((a, b) => R[a].due - R[b].due).slice(0, keys.length - 200).forEach(x => delete R[x]);
  store.set("review", R);
}
const dueReviews = () => Object.values(store.get("review") || {}).filter(r => r.due <= Date.now()).sort((a, b) => a.due - b.due);

/* today's practice */
const TODAY_N = 12;
function withTopic(tp, ty, f) { const kt = topic, ky = type; topic = tp; type = ty; try { return f(); } finally { topic = kt; type = ky; } }
function renderToday() {
  const done = store.get("todayDone") === dayKey(Date.now()), due = dueReviews().length;
  $("todayT1").textContent = done ? "Today's practice ✓ Done" : "Today's practice";
  $("todayT2").textContent = (done ? "Well done! Do another if you like · " : "About 10 minutes · mixed topics") + (due ? ` · ${due} to look at again` : "");
  if (typeof renderPlanHome === "function") renderPlanHome();
}
/* starting check-up: harder, test-style questions across every topic; one try, no hints */
const CU_PLAN = { "Number": 4, "Add and subtract": 3, "Multiply and divide": 4, "Fractions": 4, "Decimals": 2, "Money & Time": 3 };
function cuKey() { return "checkup:Y" + YEAR; }
function checkupSet() {
  const list = [];
  Object.entries(CU_PLAN).forEach(([tp, n]) => {
    if (!TREE[tp]) return;
    const subs = shuffle(Object.keys(TREE[tp]).filter(sb => sb !== "All"));
    const pools = subs.map(sb => [sb, withLevel("All", () => topicPool(tp, sb)).filter(q => q.lv === "Hard" || q.lv === "Challenge")]).filter(([, p]) => p.length);
    for (let i = 0, got = 0; got < n && pools.length && i < n * 4; i++) {
      const [sb, p] = pools[i % pools.length], q = shuffle(p.filter(x => !list.some(y => y.id === x.id)))[0];
      if (q) { list.push({ ...q, cuTopic: tp, cuSub: sb }); got++; }
    }
  });
  return list;
}
function startCheckup() {
  const set = checkupSet();
  if (!set.length) return;
  newRun(false); run.mock = true; run.checkup = true; run.deadline = 0;
  addItems(set);
  show("quiz"); renderQ();
}
function bandOf(r, n) { const p = n ? r / n : 0; return p >= .75 ? ["Strong", "strong"] : p >= .4 ? ["Getting there", "mid"] : ["Needs work", "weak"]; }
function finishCheckup() {
  const done = run.items.slice(0, run.answered).length ? run.items : [];
  const topics = {}, weakSubs = [];
  run.items.forEach(({ q }) => { const tp = q.cuTopic; if (!tp) return; topics[tp] = topics[tp] || [0, 0]; topics[tp][1] += (q.m || 1); });
  const missedIds = new Set(run.missed.map(q => q.id));
  run.items.slice(0, run.answered).forEach(({ q }) => { if (!q.cuTopic) return; if (!missedIds.has(q.id)) topics[q.cuTopic][0] += (q.m || 1); else weakSubs.push(q.cuTopic + "|" + q.cuSub); });
  run.items.slice(run.answered).forEach(({ q }) => { if (q.cuTopic) weakSubs.push(q.cuTopic + "|" + q.cuSub); });
  const res = { at: Date.now(), topics, weakSubs: [...new Set(weakSubs)], marks: run.marks, total: run.totalMarks };
  store.set(cuKey(), res);
  const hist = store.get(cuKey() + ":all") || []; hist.push(res); store.set(cuKey() + ":all", hist.slice(-12));
  $("doneTitle").textContent = "Your starting check-up";
  $("doneMsg").textContent = "Here is your map. Start with the topics that need work – your practice will focus on them.";
  renderCheckupMap($("cumap"), res); $("cumap").hidden = false;
  renderCheckupHome();
}
function renderCheckupMap(box, res) {
  box.innerHTML = "";
  const order = { weak: 0, mid: 1, strong: 2 };
  Object.entries(res.topics).map(([tp, [r, n]]) => [tp, r, n, bandOf(r, n)]).sort((a, b) => order[a[3][1]] - order[b[3][1]]).forEach(([tp, r, n, [label, cls]]) => {
    const row = document.createElement("div"); row.className = "row";
    row.innerHTML = `<span><strong></strong> <span class="muted"></span></span><span class="row" style="gap:8px;border:0;padding:0"><span class="band ${cls}">${label}</span></span>`;
    row.querySelector("strong").textContent = tp; row.querySelector(".muted").textContent = `${r} / ${n} marks`;
    const b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "ghost"; b.textContent = "Practise";
    b.onclick = () => { topic = tp; type = "All"; level = AUTO; start(); };
    row.lastElementChild.appendChild(b); box.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (res.weakSubs.length) {
    const h = document.createElement("p"); h.innerHTML = "<strong>Parts to work on:</strong>"; box.appendChild(h);
    const r = document.createElement("div"); r.className = "row"; r.style.border = "0"; r.style.padding = "0"; r.style.flexWrap = "wrap";
    res.weakSubs.forEach(k => { const [tp, sb] = k.split("|"), b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "ghost"; b.textContent = `${tp}: ${sb}`; b.onclick = () => { topic = tp; type = sb; level = AUTO; start(); }; r.appendChild(b); });
    box.appendChild(r);
  }
}
function renderCheckupHome() {
  const btn = $("checkupBtn"); if (!btn) return;
  btn.hidden = true;
  const res = store.get(cuKey());
  if (!res) { $("checkupT1").textContent = "Starting check-up"; $("checkupT2").textContent = "20 harder questions like a real test · finds your strong and weak topics"; return; }
  const weak = Object.entries(res.topics).filter(([, [r, n]]) => bandOf(r, n)[1] === "weak").map(([t]) => t);
  const days = Math.floor((Date.now() - res.at) / 864e5);
