  $("checkupT1").textContent = "Your check-up map";
  $("checkupT2").textContent = (weak.length ? "Needs work: " + weak.join(", ") : "No topics need work – great!") + ` · done ${days ? days + " days ago" : "today"} · tap to see it or do it again`;
}
function openCheckupMap() {
  const res = store.get(cuKey()); if (!res) return startCheckup();
  run = null;
  $("doneTitle").textContent = "Your check-up map";
  $("doneScore").textContent = `${res.marks} / ${res.total} marks`;
  $("doneTime").textContent = "Done on " + new Date(res.at).toLocaleDateString("en-GB");
  $("doneMsg").textContent = "Practise the topics that need work. Do the check-up again in a few weeks to see how far you have come.";
  $("retryMissed").hidden = true; $("missed").innerHTML = "";
  renderCheckupMap($("cumap"), res); $("cumap").hidden = false;
  $("again").hidden = false; $("again").textContent = "Do the check-up again"; $("again").onclick = () => { $("again").textContent = "New questions"; $("again").onclick = againDefault; startCheckup(); };
  show("done");
}
$("checkupBtn").onclick = () => (store.get(cuKey()) ? openCheckupMap() : startCheckup());
function startToday() {
  const reviews = dueReviews().slice(0, 5).map(r => ({ ...r.q }));
  const tops = TOPICS.filter(t => t !== MIXED), need = TODAY_N - reviews.length, list = [];
  const cu = store.get(cuKey()), W = {}, tw = planTopicWeights();
  tops.forEach(tp => { const x = cu && cu.topics[tp]; W[tp] = tw ? (tw[tp] ?? 1) : x ? { weak: 3, mid: 2, strong: 1 }[bandOf(x[0], x[1])[1]] : 2; });
  if (YEAR === 5) {   /* whole linked sets from a few skills, weaker skills more likely */
    SKILLS.forEach(s => { W[s.name] = tw ? (tw[s.name] ?? 1) : 2; });
    const avoid = new Set(reviews.map(r => r.id)), bag = SKILLS.map(s => s.name).filter(t => W[t] > 0);
    while (list.length < need && bag.length) {
      let r = Math.random() * bag.reduce((a, t) => a + W[t], 0), i = 0;
      while (r > W[bag[i]] && i < bag.length - 1) r -= W[bag[i++]];
      const sk = bag.splice(i, 1)[0], got = withTopic(SKILLS.find(s => s.name === sk).topic, sk, () => pickSet(3, avoid));
      got.forEach(q => avoid.add(q.id)); list.push(...got);
    }
    newRun(false); run.today = true; addItems(reviews.concat(list)); show("quiz"); renderQ(); return;
  }
  const wsum = tops.reduce((a, t) => a + W[t], 0) || 1, share = {}; let given = 0;
  tops.forEach(tp => { share[tp] = Math.floor(need * W[tp] / wsum); given += share[tp]; });
  tops.slice().sort((a, b) => W[b] - W[a]).forEach(tp => { if (given < need) { share[tp]++; given++; } });
  shuffle(tops).forEach((tp, i) => {
    const n = share[tp];
    if (!n) return;
    const lv = store.get("autoLevel:" + tp) || "Easy";
    list.push(...withTopic(tp, "All", () => withLevel(lv, () => pickSet(n, new Set(reviews.map(r => r.id))))));
  });
  newRun(false); run.today = true;
  addItems(reviews.concat(shuffle(list)));
  show("quiz"); renderQ();
}
$("todayBtn").onclick = () => planMain();

/* times tables race */
let RACE = null;
const raceMode = () => [...$("raceModes").children].find(b => b.getAttribute("aria-pressed") === "true").dataset.m;
function raceShowBest() { const b = store.get("raceBest:" + raceMode()); $("raceBest").textContent = b ? `Your best: ${b} right` : "No best score yet."; }
[...$("raceModes").children].forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { [...$("raceModes").children].forEach(c => c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", c === b)); raceShowBest(); });
function raceNext() {
  const a = R(2, 12), b = R(2, 12), m = raceMode(), div = m === "d" || (m === "mix" && Math.random() < .5);
  RACE.ans = String(div ? a : a * b); $("raceQ").textContent = div ? `${a * b} ÷ ${b} =` : `${a} × ${b} =`;
  $("raceIn").value = ""; $("raceIn").className = ""; $("raceIn").focus();
}
function raceStart() {
  RACE = { right: 0, tried: 0, end: Date.now() + 60000, lock: false };
  $("raceSetup").hidden = true; $("raceEnd").hidden = true; $("racePlay").hidden = false; $("raceScore").textContent = "0 right";
  raceNext();
  clearInterval(window.__race);
  window.__race = setInterval(() => { const left = Math.ceil((RACE.end - Date.now()) / 1000); $("raceTime").textContent = Math.max(0, left) + "s"; if (left <= 0) raceStop(); }, 200);
}
function raceStop() {
  clearInterval(window.__race); if (!RACE) return;
  const m = raceMode(), best = store.get("raceBest:" + m) || 0, nb = RACE.right > best;
  if (nb) store.set("raceBest:" + m, RACE.right);
  $("racePlay").hidden = true; $("raceEnd").hidden = false; $("raceSetup").hidden = false;
  $("raceResult").textContent = `${RACE.right} right out of ${RACE.tried}.` + (nb ? " New best!" : best ? ` Your best is ${best}.` : "");
  if (RACE.tried) { const h = getHistory(); h.push({ at: Date.now(), topic: "Times table practice", level: "All", marks: RACE.right, total: RACE.tried, right: RACE.right, answered: RACE.tried, secs: 60, types: { "Multiply & Divide · Times tables": [RACE.right, RACE.tried] } }); store.set("history", h.slice(-300)); saveProgress(); renderStreak(); }
  raceShowBest(); RACE = null;
}
$("raceIn").addEventListener("input", () => {
  if (!RACE || RACE.lock) return;
  const el = $("raceIn"); el.value = el.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
  if (el.value === RACE.ans) { RACE.right++; RACE.tried++; el.className = "good"; $("raceScore").textContent = RACE.right + " right"; RACE.lock = true; setTimeout(() => { if (RACE) { RACE.lock = false; raceNext(); } }, 150); }
  else if (el.value.length >= RACE.ans.length) { RACE.tried++; el.className = "bad"; RACE.lock = true; $("raceQ").textContent += " " + RACE.ans; setTimeout(() => { if (RACE) { RACE.lock = false; raceNext(); } }, 900); }
});
$("raceGo").onclick = raceStart; $("raceAgain").onclick = raceStart;
$("raceBtn").onclick = () => { $("racePlay").hidden = true; $("raceEnd").hidden = true; $("raceSetup").hidden = false; raceShowBest(); showPanel("raceView"); };
$("backRace").onclick = () => { clearInterval(window.__race); RACE = null; showPanel("home"); };

/* parent settings: weekly sessions and hidden features */
const S = () => ({ week: 0, hints: true, hide: {}, ...(store.get("settings") || {}) });
const FEATURES = [["todayBtn", "Today's practice"], ["ptBtn", "Practice papers"], ["pastBtn", "Past papers"], ["raceBtn", "Times table practice"], ["helpBtn", "Help cards"]];
function weekStart() { const d = new Date(); d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); d.setDate(d.getDate() - ((d.getDay() + 6) % 7)); return d.getTime(); }
function applySettings() {
  const st = S();
  FEATURES.forEach(([id]) => { const el = $(id); if (el) el.classList.toggle("off", !!st.hide[id]); });
  const n = getHistory().filter(h => h.answered > 0 && h.at >= weekStart()).length;
  $("weekGoal").hidden = !st.week;
  if (st.week) $("weekGoal").textContent = n >= st.week ? `This week: ${n} sessions – target reached` : `This week: ${n} of ${st.week} sessions`;
}
function saveSettings(st) {
  store.set("settings", st); applySettings();
  if (DB) DB.doc("settings/main").set(st).catch(() => {});
}
function renderSettings() {
  const st = S(), box = $("pSettings");
  box.innerHTML = `<h3>Practice each week</h3>
    <p class="muted small">Short, regular practice works best: about 10–15 minutes, 3 to 5 times a week.</p>
    <div class="row print-opts"><label>Sessions a week <select id="sWeek">${[0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7].map(v => `<option value="${v}"${v === st.week ? " selected" : ""}>${v ? v : "No target"}</option>`).join("")}</select></label></div>
    <h3>Show on the home screen</h3>
    <div class="settings-list">${FEATURES.map(([id, name]) => `<label><input type="checkbox" data-id="${id}"${st.hide[id] ? "" : " checked"}> ${name}</label>`).join("")}</div>
    <h3>Colour theme (${stEsc(childName() || "this child")})</h3>
    <div id="sTheme">${themeHTML()}</div>
    <h3>Help</h3>
    <div class="settings-list"><label><input type="checkbox" id="sHints"${st.hints === false ? "" : " checked"}> Show a picture hint before the second try</label></div>
    <h3>Checking questions</h3>
    <div class="settings-list"><label><input type="checkbox" id="sCheck"${st.check ? " checked" : ""}> Checking mode: show each question's number, so problems can be reported precisely</label></div>
    <p class="muted" id="sMsg"></p>
    <h3>Credits</h3>
    <p class="muted small">Some word problems are adapted from Oak National Academy resources, © Oak National Academy Limited, licensed under the <a href="https://www.nationalarchives.gov.uk/doc/open-government-licence/version/3/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open Government Licence v3.0</a>.
    Contains material developed by the Department for Education (reference DfE-00100-2020) and by the Standards and Testing Agency for 2024 national curriculum assessments, licensed under the Open Government Licence v3.0.</p>`;
  const save = () => { const n = S(); n.week = +$("sWeek").value; n.hints = $("sHints").checked; n.check = $("sCheck").checked; n.hide = {}; box.querySelectorAll("[data-id]").forEach(c => { if (!c.checked) n.hide[c.dataset.id] = true; }); saveSettings(n); $("sMsg").textContent = "Saved."; };
  box.querySelectorAll("select, input").forEach(el => el.onchange = save);
  const rt = () => { $("sTheme").innerHTML = themeHTML(); wireTheme($("sTheme"), rt); }; wireTheme($("sTheme"), rt);
}
(async () => {
  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && !DB; i++) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 250));
  if (!DB) return;
  DB.doc("settings/main").onSnapshot(d => { if (d.exists) { store.set("settings", { ...d.data() }); applySettings(); } }, () => {});
})();
renderAssigned();

/* ---------- Initial assessment: two sittings (A and B), marked automatically, with a skill map ---------- */
const ST = { level: "y56", form: 1, paper: null, ans: {}, qi: 0, secs: 0, active: false, tick: null, saveT: null };
const stEsc = s => String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
const stSkills = lv => lv === "y4" ? STARTER.sk4 : STARTER.sk56;
const stPaper = (lv, form, sit) => STARTER.papers.find(p => p.level === lv && p.form === form && p.sit === sit);
const stAttempts = () => store.get("starter:attempts") || [];
const stProgKey = id => "starter:prog:" + id;
const stBand = pct => pct >= 80 ? ["Strong", "strong"] : pct >= 50 ? ["Getting there", "mid"] : ["Needs work", "weak"];
const stDefaultLevel = () => "y56";   /* Year 5 only for now */
const stParts = P => P.qs.reduce((a, q) => a + q.parts.length, 0);
function stDefaultForm(lv) {   // finish the set that is half done; otherwise the set done fewer times, so a re-test brings new questions
  const rec = store.get("starter:pair:" + lv); if (rec && STARTER.papers.some(p => p.level === lv && p.form === rec.form)) return rec.form;
  const n = f => stAttempts().filter(a => a.level === lv && a.form === f).length;
  return n(2) < n(1) ? 2 : 1;
}
const stValue = (qi, pi) => ST.ans[qi + "." + pi];
function stPartDone(part, v) {
  const s = part.s;
  if (s.k === "multi") return s.parts.every((p, i) => p.optional || (v && String(v[i] || "").trim()));
  if (s.k === "lin") return !!(v && String(v[0] || "").trim() && String(v[1] || "").trim());
  return !!(v && String(v).trim());
}
function stScore(P, ans) {
  const skills = {}, ok = []; let marks = 0, total = 0, answered = 0;
  P.qs.forEach((q, qi) => {
    const row = [];
    q.parts.forEach((part, pi) => {
      const v = ans[qi + "." + pi], good = MK.mark(part.s, v) ? 1 : 0;
      row.push(good); marks += good; total++; if (stPartDone(part, v)) answered++;
      const k = part.sk || q.sk, s = skills[k] = skills[k] || [0, 0]; s[0] += good; s[1]++;
    });
    ok.push(row);
  });
  return { marks, total, answered, skills, ok };
}
/* the skill map: the latest sittings A and B of the same set */
function stMap(lv) {
  const att = stAttempts().filter(a => a.level === lv); if (!att.length) return null;
  const f = att[att.length - 1].form, pick = sit => { const x = att.filter(a => a.form === f && a.sit === sit); return x[x.length - 1]; };
  const parts = [pick("A"), pick("B")].filter(Boolean), skills = {}; let marks = 0, total = 0;
  parts.forEach(a => { marks += a.marks; total += a.total; Object.entries(a.skills).forEach(([k, [e, t]]) => { const s = skills[k] = skills[k] || [0, 0]; s[0] += e; s[1] += t; }); });
  return { level: lv, form: f, parts, skills, marks, total };
}
function stMapRows(map) {
  return stSkills(map.level).filter(s => map.skills[s.id]).map(s => { const [e, t] = map.skills[s.id], pct = t ? Math.round(e / t * 100) : 0; return { ...s, e, t, pct, band: stBand(pct) }; });
}
function stMapHTML(map) {
  const rows = stMapRows(map).sort((a, b) => a.pct - b.pct || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  const sits = map.parts.map(a => a.sit).join(" and ");
  return `<p class="muted small">Based on Initial assessment ${sits}${map.parts.length < 2 ? " only. Do the other one on another day for a fuller picture." : "."}</p>
    <div class="st-map">${rows.map(r => `<div class="st-row"><span><strong>${stEsc(r.name)}</strong></span><span class="st-bar" aria-hidden="true"><i class="${r.band[1]}" style="width:${r.pct}%"></i></span><span class="band ${r.band[1]}">${r.band[0]}</span><span class="sc">${r.e}/${r.t}</span>${r.pct < 80 ? `<button class="ghost" type="button" data-topic="${stEsc(r.topic)}" data-type="${stEsc(r.name)}">Practise</button>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function stWireMap(box) {
  box.querySelectorAll("button[data-topic]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { topic = b.dataset.topic; type = YEAR === 5 && b.dataset.type ? b.dataset.type : "All"; level = AUTO; start(); });
}

/* ----- start screen ----- */
function stOpen() {
  ST.level = stDefaultLevel(); ST.form = stDefaultForm(ST.level);
  $("stIntro").hidden = false; $("stExam").hidden = true; $("stResult").hidden = true; ST.active = false; clearInterval(ST.tick);
  stRenderIntro(); show("starterView");
}
function stRenderIntro() {
  const lv = ST.level, box = $("stIntro"), minutes = lv === "y4" ? 45 : 60, map = stMap(lv);
  const sit = s => {
    const P = stPaper(lv, ST.form, s), prog = store.get(stProgKey(P.id)), done = stAttempts().filter(a => a.id === P.id), last = done[done.length - 1];
    const state = prog ? `In progress · ${Object.keys(prog.ans || {}).filter(k => { const [qi, pi] = k.split(".").map(Number); return stPartDone(P.qs[qi].parts[pi], prog.ans[k]); }).length} of ${stParts(P)} parts answered` : last ? `Done ${new Date(last.at).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })} · ${last.marks} / ${last.total} marks` : `${P.qs.length} questions · ${P.marks} marks`;
    return `<div class="st-sit"><div><b>Initial assessment ${s}</b><br><span class="muted">${state}</span></div><button class="${prog || !last ? "primary" : "ghost"}" type="button" data-sit="${s}">${prog ? "Continue" : last ? "Do it again" : "Start"}</button></div>`;
  };
  box.innerHTML = `<div style="display:grid;gap:14px"><h2>Initial assessment</h2>
    <p>Two parts, Initial assessment A and Initial assessment B, with about ${stPaper(lv, ST.form, "A").qs.length} questions each in real exam order. Do them on two different days, with paper and pencil and no calculator. Each takes up to ${minutes} minutes. The app notes how long you take but never stops you.</p>
    <p class="muted">When both are done you can see your progress in each topic. Your answers are saved as you go, so you can stop and carry on later.</p>
    ${sit("A")}${sit("B")}
    ${map ? `<h3>Your progress</h3><div id="stMapBox">${stMapHTML(map)}</div>` : ""}</div>`;
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-lv]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { ST.level = b.dataset.lv; store.set("starter:level:" + YEAR, ST.level); ST.form = stDefaultForm(ST.level); stRenderIntro(); });
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-f]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { ST.form = +b.dataset.f; stRenderIntro(); });
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-sit]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => stStart(b.dataset.sit));
  if (map) stWireMap($("stMapBox"));
}

/* ----- the exam ----- */
function stStart(sit) {
  const P = stPaper(ST.level, ST.form, sit), prog = store.get(stProgKey(P.id));
  ST.paper = P; ST.ans = prog && prog.ans ? prog.ans : {}; ST.secs = prog ? prog.secs || 0 : 0; ST.qi = prog ? Math.min(prog.qi || 0, P.qs.length - 1) : 0; ST.active = true;
  $("stIntro").hidden = true; $("stResult").hidden = true; $("stExam").hidden = false; $("stConfirm").hidden = true;
  $("stTitle").textContent = "Initial assessment " + sit; clearInterval(ST.tick);
  ST.tick = setInterval(() => { if (!document.hidden && !$("starterView").hidden && ST.active) { ST.secs++; stTimer(); } }, 1000);
  stNav(); stShowQ(); window.scrollTo(0, 0);
}
const stTimer = () => { $("stTimer").textContent = fmtTime(ST.secs); };
function stSave() { if (!ST.paper) return; store.set(stProgKey(ST.paper.id), { ans: ST.ans, secs: ST.secs, qi: ST.qi, at: Date.now() }); }
function stDirty() { clearTimeout(ST.saveT); ST.saveT = setTimeout(stSave, 400); stNavState(); }
function stNav() {
  const nav = $("stNav"); nav.innerHTML = "";
  ST.paper.qs.forEach((q, i) => { const b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.textContent = q.n; b.setAttribute("aria-label", "Question " + q.n); b.onclick = () => { ST.qi = i; stShowQ(); stSave(); }; nav.appendChild(b); });
  stNavState();
}
function stNavState() {
  const P = ST.paper; let answered = 0, all = 0;
  [...$("stNav").children].forEach((b, i) => {
    const q = P.qs[i], n = q.parts.filter((p, pi) => stPartDone(p, stValue(i, pi))).length; answered += n; all += q.parts.length;
    b.className = (n === q.parts.length ? "done" : n ? "part" : "") + (i === ST.qi ? " cur" : "");
  });
  $("stCount").textContent = `${answered} of ${all} parts answered`; $("stBar").style.width = Math.round(answered / all * 100) + "%"; stTimer();
}
function nudge(el) { el.classList.add("nudge"); setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove("nudge"), 400); }
function stField(qi, pi, spec, idx, label, part) {
  const wrap = document.createElement("div"); wrap.className = "st-sub";
  if (label) { const l = document.createElement("span"); l.className = "st-sublab"; l.textContent = label; wrap.appendChild(l); }
  const k = qi + "." + pi, cur = () => { const v = ST.ans[k]; return idx == null ? v : Array.isArray(v) ? v[idx] : undefined; };
  const put = val => { if (idx == null) ST.ans[k] = val; else { const a = Array.isArray(ST.ans[k]) ? ST.ans[k].slice() : []; a[idx] = val; ST.ans[k] = a; } stDirty(); };
  if (spec.k === "ch") {
    const g = document.createElement("div"); g.className = "st-opts"; g.setAttribute("role", "group");
    spec.o.forEach(o => { const b = document.createElement("button"); b.type = "button"; b.className = "st-opt"; b.innerHTML = fracHTML(o); b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(cur() === o));
      b.onclick = () => { put(cur() === o ? "" : o); [...g.children].forEach(c => c.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(c === b && cur() === o))); }; g.appendChild(b); });
    wrap.appendChild(g); return wrap;
  }
  if (spec.k === "f" && idx == null) {
    const f = document.createElement("div"); f.className = "st-field fracin row";
    const [tn, td] = String(spec.v).split("/").map(Number), whole = !(td && Math.abs(tn) < td);   /* no whole-number box when the answer is less than 1 */
    f.innerHTML = `<input class="fw" type="text" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="Whole number (leave empty if none)" placeholder=" "${whole ? "" : " hidden"}><span class="fracbox"><input type="text" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="Top number"><span class="fline"></span><input type="text" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="Bottom number"></span>`;
    const [w, n, d] = f.querySelectorAll("input"), v0 = String(cur() || ""), m0 = /^\s*(?:(\d+)\s+)?(\d+)\s*\/\s*(\d+)\s*$/.exec(v0);
    if (m0) { w.value = m0[1] || ""; n.value = m0[2]; d.value = m0[3]; } else if (/^\s*\d+\s*$/.test(v0)) w.value = v0.trim();
    const upd = () => { const W = w.value.trim(), N = n.value.trim(), D = d.value.trim(); put(N || D ? (W ? W + " " : "") + N + "/" + D : W); };
    [w, n, d].forEach((el, i, all) => { el.oninput = () => { const v = el.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); if (v !== el.value) { el.value = v; nudge(el); } upd(); }; el.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); (all[i + 1] || el).focus(); } }; });
    wrap.appendChild(f);
    const hint = document.createElement("div"); hint.className = "muted"; hint.style.fontSize = ".85rem"; hint.textContent = whole ? "Whole number (if any), then top and bottom of the fraction." : "Top and bottom of the fraction."; wrap.appendChild(hint);
    return wrap;
  }
  const f = document.createElement("label"); f.className = "st-field";
  if (part.pre && idx == null) f.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", `<span class="pre">${stEsc(part.pre)}</span>`);
  const inp = document.createElement("input"); inp.type = "text"; inp.autocomplete = "off"; inp.autocapitalize = "none"; inp.spellcheck = false;
  const plain = spec.k === "n" && /^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(spec.v) && !spec.alt; inp.inputMode = plain && !spec.au ? "decimal" : "text";
  if (part.ph && idx == null) inp.placeholder = part.ph;
  inp.setAttribute("aria-label", `Answer, question ${ST.paper.qs[qi].n} part ${"abcdefg"[pi]}${label ? ", " + label : ""}`);
  inp.value = cur() || "";
  /* number answers take no letters: only the signs that answer can need */
  const bad = { n: /[^0-9.,\-− ]/g, t: spec.h12 ? /[^0-9:. apm]/gi : /[^0-9:.]/g, c: /[^0-9,\-−() ]/g, list: /[^0-9.,;\-− ]/g, dig: /[^0-9 ]/g }[spec.k];
  inp.oninput = () => { if (bad) { const v = inp.value.replace(bad, ""); if (v !== inp.value) { inp.value = v; nudge(inp); } } put(inp.value); };
  inp.onkeydown = e => { if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); const all = [...$("stQ").querySelectorAll("input")], i = all.indexOf(inp); if (all[i + 1]) all[i + 1].focus(); else inp.blur(); } };
  f.appendChild(inp);
  if (part.suf && idx == null) f.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", `<span class="suf">${stEsc(part.suf)}</span>`);
  wrap.appendChild(f); return wrap;
}
function stShowQ() {
  const P = ST.paper, q = P.qs[ST.qi], box = $("stQ"); $("stConfirm").hidden = true;
  let h = `<h3 class="st-qh">Question ${q.n} <span class="muted">· ${q.parts.length} mark${q.parts.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}</span></h3>`;
  q.intro.forEach(l => { h += `<p class="st-intro">${fracHTML(l)}</p>`; });
  if (q.table) h += `<table class="st-table">${q.table.map(r => "<tr>" + r.map(c => `<td>${fracHTML(String(c))}</td>`).join("") + "</tr>").join("")}</table>`;
  if (q.fig) h += `<div class="st-fig"><img src="${q.fig}" alt="Diagram for question ${q.n}"></div>`;
  if (q.pic) h += `<div class="picbox">${drawPic(q.pic)}</div>`;
  box.innerHTML = h + `<div id="stParts"></div>` + reportUI(q.id); wireReport(box);
  q.parts.forEach((part, pi) => {
    const d = document.createElement("div"); d.className = "st-part";
    d.innerHTML = `<span class="st-lab">(${"abcdefg"[pi]})</span><div class="st-ptext">${fracHTML(part.t)}</div><div class="st-in"></div>`;
    const inn = d.querySelector(".st-in"), s = part.s;
    if (s.k === "multi") s.parts.forEach((sp, i) => inn.appendChild(stField(ST.qi, pi, sp, i, (part.labels || [])[i], part)));
    else if (s.k === "lin") [0, 1].forEach(i => inn.appendChild(stField(ST.qi, pi, { k: "n", v: "0" }, i, part.labels[i], part)));
    else inn.appendChild(stField(ST.qi, pi, s, null, null, part));
    $("stParts").appendChild(d);
  });
  const last = ST.qi === P.qs.length - 1;
  $("stPrev").disabled = ST.qi === 0; $("stNext").textContent = last ? "Finish" : "Next";
  $("stNext").onclick = last ? stAskFinish : () => { ST.qi++; stShowQ(); stSave(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); };
  $("stPrev").onclick = () => { if (ST.qi > 0) { ST.qi--; stShowQ(); stSave(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); } };
  stNavState();
}
function stUnanswered() { const P = ST.paper; let n = 0; P.qs.forEach((q, qi) => q.parts.forEach((p, pi) => { if (!stPartDone(p, stValue(qi, pi))) n++; })); return n; }
function stAskFinish() {
  const n = stUnanswered();
  $("stConfirmMsg").textContent = n ? `${n} part${n > 1 ? "s are" : " is"} not answered yet. Finish anyway? Unanswered parts score no marks.` : "All parts are answered. Finish this assessment?";
  $("stConfirmYes").textContent = "Finish and mark"; $("stConfirmYes").onclick = stFinish; $("stConfirmNo").textContent = "Keep going"; $("stConfirmNo").onclick = () => { $("stConfirm").hidden = true; };
  $("stConfirm").hidden = false; $("stConfirm").scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
}
function stAskLeave() {
  $("stConfirmMsg").textContent = "Leave this assessment? Your answers are saved, so you can carry on later.";
  $("stConfirmYes").textContent = "Leave"; $("stConfirmYes").onclick = () => { stSave(); ST.active = false; clearInterval(ST.tick); stOpen(); };
  $("stConfirmNo").textContent = "Keep going"; $("stConfirmNo").onclick = () => { $("stConfirm").hidden = true; };
  $("stConfirm").hidden = false; $("stConfirm").scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
}
function stFinish() {
  const P = ST.paper, sc = stScore(P, ST.ans); ST.active = false; clearInterval(ST.tick); clearTimeout(ST.saveT);
  const att = { at: Date.now(), id: P.id, level: P.level, form: P.form, sit: P.sit, secs: ST.secs, marks: sc.marks, total: sc.total, answered: sc.answered, skills: sc.skills, ans: ST.ans, ok: sc.ok };
  const all = stAttempts(); all.push(att); store.set("starter:attempts", all.slice(-24)); store.set(stProgKey(P.id), null);
  const pk = "starter:pair:" + P.level, rec = store.get(pk);
  if (rec && rec.form === P.form && !rec.done.includes(P.sit)) store.set(pk, rec.done.length + 1 >= 2 ? null : { form: rec.form, done: [...rec.done, P.sit] }); else store.set(pk, { form: P.form, done: [P.sit] });
  const hist = getHistory(), types = {}; Object.entries(sc.skills).forEach(([k, v]) => { const s = stSkills(P.level).find(x => x.id === k); types["Initial assessment · " + (s ? s.name : k)] = v; });
  hist.push({ at: att.at, topic: "Initial assessment " + P.sit, level: "All", mock: true, marks: sc.marks, total: sc.total, right: sc.marks, answered: sc.answered, secs: ST.secs, types }); store.set("history", hist.slice(-300));
  stSaveCheckup(P.level); saveProgress(); renderStreak(); stResult(att);
}
/* feed the map into Today's practice, which already weights topics by a check-up result */
function stSaveCheckup(lv) {
  const map = stMap(lv); if (!map) return; const topics = {};
  stMapRows(map).forEach(r => { const t = topics[r.topic] = topics[r.topic] || [0, 0]; t[0] += r.e; t[1] += r.t; });
  store.set(cuKey(), { at: Date.now(), topics, weakSubs: [], marks: map.marks, total: map.total });
}
function stResult(att) {
  const P = STARTER.papers.find(p => p.id === att.id), map = stMap(att.level), box = $("stResult"), pct = att.total ? Math.round(att.marks / att.total * 100) : 0;
  const weak = stMapRows(map).filter(r => r.pct < 80).sort((a, b) => a.pct - b.pct).slice(0, 3).map(r => r.name);
  $("stIntro").hidden = true; $("stExam").hidden = true; box.hidden = false;
  const other = att.sit === "A" ? "B" : "A", otherDone = map.parts.some(a => a.sit === other);
  box.innerHTML = `<div style="display:grid;gap:14px"><h2>Initial assessment ${att.sit} finished</h2>
    <p class="score">${att.marks} / ${att.total}</p><p class="muted">${pct}% · time ${fmtTime(att.secs)}${att.secs > (att.level === "y4" ? 45 : 60) * 60 ? " (over the " + (att.level === "y4" ? 45 : 60) + "-minute exam time)" : ""}</p>
    <p>${otherDone ? (weak.length ? "Start with " + weak.join(", ") + ". Today's practice now leans towards these topics." : "No weak skills. Keep practising a mix.") : `Do Initial assessment ${other} on another day to complete your skill map.`}</p>
    <h3>Your progress</h3><div id="stMapBox2">${stMapHTML(map)}</div>
    <div class="row"><button class="primary" id="stAgain" type="button">${otherDone ? "Back to Initial assessment" : "Do Initial assessment " + other}</button><button class="ghost" id="stHome" type="button">Home</button></div>
    <h3>Your answers</h3><div class="st-rev" id="stRev">${stReviewHTML(P, att)}</div></div>`;
  stWireMap($("stMapBox2"));
  $("stAgain").onclick = () => { if (!otherDone) { ST.level = att.level; ST.form = att.form; stStart(other); } else stOpen(); };
  $("stHome").onclick = () => show("home"); window.scrollTo(0, 0);
}
function stReviewHTML(P, att) {
  const show1 = v => Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter(x => x != null && x !== "").join(" · ") : v;
  return P.qs.map((q, qi) => {
    const e = att.ok[qi].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    return `<details><summary>Question ${q.n} · ${e} / ${q.parts.length}</summary>${q.parts.map((p, pi) => {
      const mine = show1(att.ans[qi + "." + pi]);
      return `<div class="rp"><div class="${att.ok[qi][pi] ? "ok" : "no"}">${att.ok[qi][pi] ? "✓" : "✗"}</div><div>${fracHTML(p.t)}<br><span class="ans">${mine ? "Your answer: " + fracHTML(String(mine)) : "No answer"}${att.ok[qi][pi] ? "" : " · Correct answer: " + fracHTML(p.a)}</span></div></div>`;
    }).join("")}</details>`;
  }).join("");
}
/* parent progress tab */
function starterParentHTML() {
  const att = stAttempts().slice().reverse();
  if (!att.length) return "";
  const d = t => new Date(t).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
  const lvName = l => l === "y4" ? "Year 4" : "Year 5–6";
  const shown = [...new Set(att.map(a => a.level))].map(lv => { const m = stMap(lv); return m ? `<h4>Your progress</h4>${stMapHTML(m)}` : ""; }).join("");
  return `<h3>Initial assessment</h3>${shown}<table class="ptable"><tr><th>Date</th><th>Assessment</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th class="n">Time</th></tr>${att.slice(0, 8).map(a => `<tr><td>${d(a.at)}</td><td>Initial assessment ${a.sit}</td><td class="n">${a.marks}/${a.total}</td><td class="n">${fmtTime(a.secs)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</table>`;
}
function renderStarterHome() {
  const att = stAttempts(), lv = stDefaultLevel(), map = stMap(lv);
  if (!map) { $("starterT1").textContent = att.length ? "Initial assessment" : "Initial assessment: start here"; $("starterT2").textContent = "Initial assessment A and B · finds your strong and weak skills · then you know what to practise"; return; }
  const rows = stMapRows(map).filter(r => r.pct < 80).sort((a, b) => a.pct - b.pct).slice(0, 3).map(r => r.name);
  $("starterT1").textContent = "Your progress";
  $("starterT2").textContent = (map.parts.length < 2 ? "Initial assessment " + (map.parts[0].sit === "A" ? "B" : "A") + " still to do · " : "") + "tap to open";
}
/* ----- three plans: papers / papers and topics / topics first ----- */
const PLANS = { papers: ["Papers", "a paper every week, then go over what went wrong", 7], mixed: ["Papers and topics", "daily topic practice on your weaker skills, a paper every 2 weeks", 14], topics: ["Topics first", "daily practice on the skills that need work, a topic test each week, a paper each month", 28] };
function examResults() { return profResults().filter(r => r.total && (r.exam || (r.att && r.att.level === "y56"))); }
function planAuto() { const r = examResults().slice(0, 2); if (!r.length) return null; const p = r.reduce((a, x) => a + x.marks / x.total, 0) / r.length * 100; return p >= 75 ? "papers" : p >= 50 ? "mixed" : "topics"; }
function planGroup() { const o = (store.get("plan") || {}).group; return PLANS[o] ? o : planAuto(); }
function planTopicWeights() {
  const map = stMap(stDefaultLevel()); if (!map) return null;
  const by = {}; stMapRows(map).forEach(r => { const k = YEAR === 5 ? r.name : r.topic; (by[k] = by[k] || []).push(r.pct); });
  const g = planGroup(), W = {};
  Object.entries(by).forEach(([tp, a]) => { const p = a.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0) / a.length; W[tp] = p < 50 ? 4 : p < 75 ? 2 : g === "topics" ? 0 : 1; });
  return W;
}
function weakestTopic() { const map = stMap(stDefaultLevel()); if (!map) return null; const r = stMapRows(map).filter(x => x.topic in MAIN).sort((a, b) => a.pct - b.pct)[0]; return r ? r.topic : null; }
function nextPaper() {   /* the first open paper not yet tried; if all open ones are tried, the open one with the lowest best score */
  const done = new Set(getHistory().map(h => h.topic)), open = PRACTICE_TESTS.filter(([c]) => QUESTIONS.some(q => q.paper === c)).slice(0, papersOpenTo());
  return open.find(([c, n]) => !done.has(n)) || open[open.length - 1];
}
function startPaper() { const p = nextPaper(); if (!p) return openPapers("pt"); const retake = !!firstTry(p[1]); startMock({ items: retake ? twinPaper(p[0]) : QUESTIONS.filter(q => q.paper === p[0]), minutes: 60 }); run.paperName = p[1]; run.retake = retake; renderQ(); }
function startTopicTest(tp) {
  const keep = YEAR, items = [];   /* the Year 5 bank is thin in places: top up from Year 6, then Year 4 */
  try { [YEAR, YEAR + 1, YEAR - 1].forEach(y => { if (items.length >= 15) return; YEAR = y; items.push(...withTopic(tp, "All", () => withLevel("All", () => pickSet(15 - items.length, new Set(items.map(q => q.id)))))); }); } finally { YEAR = keep; }
  if (!items.length) return; startMock({ items, minutes: 20 }); run.paperName = "Topic test: " + tp; renderQ();
}
function paperDue(g) { const last = examResults()[0]; return !last || Date.now() - last.at >= PLANS[g][2] * 864e5 - 432e5; }
function planMain() { const g = planGroup(); if (g === "papers" && paperDue(g)) return startPaper(); startToday(); }
function renderPlanHome() {
  const g = planGroup(), line = $("planLine"), b2 = $("plan2Btn"), t1 = $("todayT1"), t2 = $("todayT2");
  document.body.classList.toggle("plan-papers", g === "papers");
  $("topicsToggle").hidden = g !== "papers";
  $("topicsToggle").textContent = document.body.classList.contains("show-topics") ? "Hide topics" : "Practise a topic";
  b2.hidden = true;
  if (!g) { line.hidden = true; $("starterBtn").classList.remove("off"); return; }
  $("starterBtn").classList.add("off");   // skill map now lives in My progress
  line.hidden = false; line.innerHTML = `Your plan: <b>${PLANS[g][0]}</b> · ${PLANS[g][1]}`;
  const due = paperDue(g), np = nextPaper(), last = examResults()[0];
  const nextOn = last ? new Date(last.at + PLANS[g][2] * 864e5).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "short" }) : "";
  const paperT1 = np ? np[1] : "A past paper", paperT2 = np ? "60 minutes · 60 marks · no calculator" : "All exam-like papers done · print a real CSSE paper";
  if (g === "papers") {
    if (due) { t1.textContent = "This week's paper: " + paperT1; t2.textContent = paperT2; }
    else { t1.textContent = "Go over what went wrong"; t2.textContent = "Practice on the skills you lost marks on · next paper on " + nextOn; }
    return;
  }
  if (due) { b2.hidden = false; $("plan2T1").textContent = "Paper due: " + paperT1; $("plan2T2").textContent = paperT2; b2.onclick = startPaper; }
  else if (g === "topics") {
    const tp = weakestTopic();
    if (tp) { b2.hidden = false; $("plan2T1").textContent = "Topic test: " + tp; $("plan2T2").textContent = "15 questions · 20 minutes · your weakest topic"; b2.onclick = () => startTopicTest(tp); }
  }
  if (!store.get("todayDone") || store.get("todayDone") !== dayKey(Date.now())) t2.textContent = "About 10 minutes · mostly your weaker skills";
}
$("topicsToggle").onclick = () => { document.body.classList.toggle("show-topics"); renderPlanHome(); };
/* ----- child profile ----- */
function profResults() {
  const out = [];
  stAttempts().forEach(a => out.push({ at: a.at, name: "Initial assessment " + a.sit, marks: a.marks, total: a.total, secs: a.secs, att: a }));
  getHistory().filter(h => h.mock && !h.retake && !/^Initial assessment|^Starter/.test(h.topic)).forEach(h => out.push({ at: h.at, name: h.topic, marks: h.marks, total: h.total, secs: h.secs, exam: !!h.paper || /^Practice test|^Exam-like/.test(h.topic) }));
  return out.sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
}
/* estimate like a school predicted grade: exam-level results give a score out of 60; otherwise use year-level work */
function profPredict(res) {
  const pc = r => r.marks / r.total * 100, avg = a => Math.round(a.reduce((x, r) => x + pc(r), 0) / a.length);
  const exam = res.filter(r => r.total && (r.exam || (r.att && r.att.level === "y56"))).slice(0, 3);
  const year = res.filter(r => r.total && r.att && r.att.level === "y4").slice(0, 2);
  const src = exam.length ? exam : year; if (!src.length) return "";
  const p = avg(src), band = p >= 85 ? "Ahead" : p >= 70 ? "On track" : "Working towards";
  const est = exam.length ? `<span>Estimated CSSE score today: <b class="big">${Math.round(p * 0.6)} / 60</b> (${p}%)</span><span class="muted">From the last ${exam.length} exam-level paper${exam.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}.</span>`
    : `<span>Working at <b>${p}%</b> on Year 4 questions.</span><span class="muted">Do an exam-like or past paper for an estimated score out of 60.</span>`;
  return `<div class="pred"><span class="muted">Predicted, like a school grade (if progress carries on at this rate)</span><b class="big">${band}</b>${est}</div>`;
}
function profTrendSVG(rows) {
  const pts = rows.slice().reverse().filter(r => r.total).slice(-12); if (pts.length < 2) return "";
  const W = 320, H = 130, x = i => 30 + i * (W - 44) / (pts.length - 1), y = p => 14 + (100 - p) * (H - 34) / 100;
  const line = pts.map((r, i) => `${i ? "L" : "M"}${x(i).toFixed(1)} ${y(r.marks / r.total * 100).toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
  return `<svg class="trend" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" role="img" aria-label="Scores over time">
    
    <line x1="30" x2="30" y1="${y(100)}" y2="${y(0)}" stroke="#c9d3d8"/><text x="24" y="${y(100) + 4}" text-anchor="end">100</text><text x="24" y="${y(0) + 4}" text-anchor="end">0</text>
    <path d="${line}" fill="none" stroke="var(--accent)" stroke-width="2.5"/>${pts.map((r, i) => `<circle cx="${x(i)}" cy="${y(r.marks / r.total * 100)}" r="4" fill="var(--accent)"><title>${stEsc(r.name)}: ${Math.round(r.marks / r.total * 100)}%</title></circle>`).join("")}</svg>`;
}
function profSkillTrend(lv) {
  /* compare whole sets (sittings A+B of form 1 vs form 2), not one sitting with the other */
  const sets = {}; stAttempts().filter(a => a.level === lv).forEach(a => { const s = sets[a.form] = sets[a.form] || { at: 0, sk: {} }; s.at = Math.max(s.at, a.at); Object.entries(a.skills).forEach(([k, [e, t]]) => { const v = s.sk[k] = s.sk[k] || [0, 0]; v[0] += e; v[1] += t; }); });
  const last = {}, prev = {};
  Object.values(sets).sort((x, y) => x.at - y.at).forEach(s => Object.entries(s.sk).forEach(([k, [e, t]]) => { if (!t) return; if (k in last) prev[k] = last[k]; last[k] = e / t; }));
  return k => !(k in prev) ? "" : last[k] > prev[k] + 0.05 ? ' <span class="up" title="Better than last time">↑</span>' : last[k] < prev[k] - 0.05 ? ' <span class="down" title="Lower than last time">↓</span>' : "";
}

/* ----- heat map: every topic and part, from the Initial assessment and practice ----- */
function heatData() {
  const map = stMap(stDefaultLevel()), byId = {};
  if (map) stMapRows(map).forEach(r => { byId[r.id] = [r.e, r.t]; });
  const prac = {};
  getHistory().forEach(h => Object.entries(h.types || {}).forEach(([k, [r, n]]) => { const nm = k.split(" · ")[0]; const id = SKILL_BY_NAME[nm]; if (!id) return; const v = prac[id] = prac[id] || [0, 0]; v[0] += r; v[1] += n; }));
  return Object.entries(MAIN).concat(MIXED_ONLY.length ? [[MIXED, MIXED_ONLY]] : []).map(([main, ids]) => ({ main, cells: ids.map(id => {
    const a = byId[id] || [0, 0], b = prac[id] || [0, 0], t = a[1] + b[1];
    /* the assessment counts double: it is exam conditions with no second go */
    const pct = t ? Math.round((2 * a[0] + b[0]) / (2 * a[1] + b[1]) * 100) : null;
    return { id, name: SKILL_NAME[id], main, pct, n: t, band: pct == null ? "hnone" : HEAT_BANDS.find(([m]) => pct >= m)[1] };
  }) }));
}
function heatHTML(rows, small) {
  const sum = r => { const t = r.cells.filter(c => c.pct != null); return small || !t.length ? "" : ` <span class="muted small">· ${Math.round(t.reduce((a, c) => a + c.pct, 0) / t.length)}% right · ${t.length} of ${r.cells.length} part${r.cells.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} tried</span>`; };
  return `<div class="heat${small ? " small" : ""}">${rows.map(r => `<div class="hrow"><b>${stEsc(r.main)}${sum(r)}</b><div class="hcells">${r.cells.map(c =>
    `<button type="button" class="hcell ${c.band}" data-topic="${stEsc(c.main)}" data-type="${stEsc(c.name)}" title="${stEsc(c.name)}: ${c.pct == null ? "not tried yet" : c.pct + "%"}"${small ? " tabindex=\"-1\"" : ""}><span>${stEsc(c.name)}</span><span class="p">${c.pct == null ? "–" : c.pct + "%"}</span><span class="q">${c.n ? c.n + " question" + (c.n > 1 ? "s" : "") : "not tried yet"}</span></button>`).join("")}</div></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
const HEAT_LEGEND = `<div class="hlegend"><span><i class="h0"></i>under 30%</span><span><i class="h1"></i>30–49%</span><span><i class="h2"></i>50–69%</span><span><i class="h3"></i>70–84%</span><span><i class="h4"></i>85% and over</span><span><i class="hnone"></i>not tried</span></div>`;
function weakSkills(n) {
  const cells = heatData().flatMap(r => r.cells).filter(c => c.main !== MIXED);
  const tried = cells.filter(c => c.pct != null).sort((a, b) => a.pct - b.pct), untried = cells.filter(c => c.pct == null);
  return tried.filter(c => c.pct < 85).concat(untried).slice(0, n);
}
/* this week's plan for the child: depends on the plan (papers / mixed / topics), weak skills and the test date */
function weekPlan() {
  const g = planGroup() || "topics", plan = store.get("plan") || {}, mins = plan.mins || 20, weak = weakSkills(6).map(c => c.name);
  const weeks = plan.testDate ? Math.ceil((new Date(plan.testDate) - Date.now()) / (7 * 864e5)) : null, close = weeks != null && weeks <= 4 && weeks >= 0;
  const w = i => weak.length ? weak[i % weak.length] : "a mix of topics", due = paperDue(g), wt = weakestTopic() || (weak[0] ? MAIN_OF[SKILL_BY_NAME[weak[0]]] : "your weakest topic");
  const T = { practise: (a, b) => `Practise ${a}${b && b !== a ? " and " + b : ""} · ${mins} min`, mix: `Today's practice (mixed) · ${mins} min`,
    paper: "Practice paper · 60 min, then mark it", review: "Go over mistakes from the paper · 15 min", rest: "Rest day", test: `Topic test: ${wt} · 20 min`, mistakes: "Review my mistakes · 10 min" };
  const P = g === "papers" || close ? [T.mix, T.practise(w(0)), T.mistakes, T.practise(w(1)), T.rest, T.paper, T.review]
    : g === "mixed" ? [T.practise(w(0), w(1)), T.mix, T.practise(w(2), w(3)), T.mix, T.rest, due ? T.paper : T.test, T.mistakes]
    : [T.practise(w(0), w(1)), T.practise(w(2), w(3)), T.practise(w(4), w(0)), T.mistakes, T.practise(w(1), w(2)), T.test, T.rest];
  const start = new Date(); start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0); const dow = (start.getDay() + 6) % 7;   /* Monday = 0 */
  return { g, close, weeks, days: P.map((txt, i) => { const d = new Date(start.getTime() + (i - dow) * 864e5); return { d, txt, today: i === dow }; }) };
}
function weekPlanHTML(wp) {
  const name = PLANS[wp.g] ? PLANS[wp.g][0] : "";
  return `<p class="muted small">Plan: <b>${name}</b>${wp.close ? " · the test is close, so a paper every week" : ""}${wp.weeks != null && wp.weeks >= 0 ? ` · ${wp.weeks} week${wp.weeks === 1 ? "" : "s"} to the test` : ""}. It changes as results come in.</p>
    <ol class="wplan">${wp.days.map(x => `<li class="${x.today ? "today" : ""}"><b>${x.d.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { weekday: "short", day: "numeric" })}</b><span>${stEsc(x.txt)}</span></li>`).join("")}</ol>`;
}
/* ----- colour theme (per child) ----- */
const ACCENTS = { teal: "#0f7c8c", blue: "#1d5fd1", purple: "#6b46c1", green: "#1f7a3a", orange: "#c2510c", pink: "#c0306b" };
function applyTheme() {
  const t = store.get("theme") || {}, r = document.documentElement;
  if (t.accent && ACCENTS[t.accent]) r.dataset.accent = t.accent; else delete r.dataset.accent;
  if (["light", "dark", "cream", "contrast"].includes(t.mode)) r.dataset.theme = t.mode; else delete r.dataset.theme;
  if (t.size === "l" || t.size === "xl") r.dataset.size = t.size; else delete r.dataset.size;
  if (t.font === "easy") r.dataset.font = "easy"; else delete r.dataset.font;
  if (t.cb) r.dataset.cb = "1"; else delete r.dataset.cb;
  if (t.calm) r.dataset.calm = "1"; else delete r.dataset.calm;
  const m = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]'); if (m) m.content = ACCENTS[t.accent] || ACCENTS.teal;
}
function themeHTML() {
  const t = store.get("theme") || {};
  return `<div style="display:grid;gap:8px"><span><b>Colours</b></span>
    <div class="swatches" role="group" aria-label="Colour">${Object.entries(ACCENTS).map(([k, c]) => `<button type="button" class="swatch" data-accent="${k}" style="background:${c}" aria-label="${k}" aria-pressed="${(t.accent || "teal") === k}"></button>`).join("")}</div>
    <span><b>Background</b></span>
    <div class="chips" role="group" aria-label="Background">${[["", "Automatic"], ["light", "Light"], ["dark", "Dark"], ["cream", "Cream"], ["contrast", "High contrast"]].map(([k, l]) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-mode="${k}" aria-pressed="${(t.mode || "") === k}">${l}</button>`).join("")}</div>
    <span><b>Text size</b></span>
    <div class="chips" role="group" aria-label="Text size">${[["", "Normal"], ["l", "Large"], ["xl", "Extra large"]].map(([k, l]) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-size="${k}" aria-pressed="${(t.size || "") === k}">${l}</button>`).join("")}</div>
    <span><b>Letters</b></span>
    <div class="chips" role="group" aria-label="Letters">${[["", "Standard"], ["easy", "Easy to read (wider spacing)"]].map(([k, l]) => `<button type="button" class="chip" data-font="${k}" aria-pressed="${(t.font || "") === k}">${l}</button>`).join("")}</div>
    <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" data-flag="cb"${t.cb ? " checked" : ""}> Colour-blind friendly (blue for right, orange for wrong)</label>
    <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" data-flag="calm"${t.calm ? " checked" : ""}> Less movement on screen</label></div>`;
}
function wireTheme(box, after) {
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-accent]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { store.set("theme", { ...(store.get("theme") || {}), accent: b.dataset.accent }); applyTheme(); after(); });
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-mode]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { store.set("theme", { ...(store.get("theme") || {}), mode: b.dataset.mode }); applyTheme(); after(); });
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-size]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { store.set("theme", { ...(store.get("theme") || {}), size: b.dataset.size }); applyTheme(); after(); });
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-font]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { store.set("theme", { ...(store.get("theme") || {}), font: b.dataset.font }); applyTheme(); after(); });
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-flag]").forEach(b => b.onchange = () => { store.set("theme", { ...(store.get("theme") || {}), [b.dataset.flag]: b.checked }); applyTheme(); after(); });
}
applyTheme();
/* ----- parents: one card per child ----- */
/* weekly note for parents: what was done, first-try paper scores, what to practise next */
function weeklyNote(k) {
  return asChild(k.id, () => {
    const now = Date.now(), week = getHistory().filter(h => h.at > now - 7 * 864e5 && h.answered > 0);
    const mins = Math.round(week.reduce((a, h) => a + (h.secs || 0), 0) / 60), qs = week.reduce((a, h) => a + (h.answered || 0), 0);
    const right = week.filter(h => !h.mock).reduce((a, h) => a + (h.right || 0), 0), tried = week.filter(h => !h.mock).reduce((a, h) => a + (h.answered || 0), 0);
    const days = new Set(week.map(h => dayKey(h.at))).size, papers = week.filter(h => h.paper && !h.retake && h.total);
    const weak = getHistory().length || stAttempts().length ? weakSkills(3).map(c => c.name) : [], nm = k.name || "Your child";
    const lines = [`${nm}: this week in Mind the Gap 11+`,
      `${days} day${days === 1 ? "" : "s"} of practice, about ${mins} minutes, ${qs} questions answered${tried ? `, ${Math.round(right / tried * 100)}% right in topic practice` : ""}.`,
      papers.length ? "Practice papers (first try): " + papers.map(h => `${h.topic.replace("Practice test", "Paper")} ${h.marks}/${h.total} (${Math.round(h.marks / h.total * 100)}%)`).join("; ") + "." : "No practice paper this week.",
      weak.length ? "Practise next: " + weak.join(", ") + "." : "",
      days < 3 ? "Tip: short sessions on 4 or 5 days a week work better than one long one." : "Good habit: keep the short daily sessions going."].filter(Boolean);
    return lines.join("\n");
  });
}
function renderKids() {
  const box = $("pKids"), kids = children();
  box.innerHTML = `<p class="muted small">Each child's estimated score, this week's practice and scores for every topic. Tap a child to open their full progress and plan.</p>` + kids.map(k => asChild(k.id, () => {
    const res = profResults(), hist = getHistory(), week = hist.filter(h => h.at > Date.now() - 7 * 864e5), mins = Math.round(week.reduce((a, h) => a + (h.secs || 0), 0) / 60);
    const weak = weakSkills(3).map(c => c.name), g = planGroup();
    return `<div class="kidcard"><h3>${avatarHTML(k, "kidav")}${stEsc(k.name || "Child 1")}</h3>${profPredict(res) || '<p class="muted">No results yet. Start with the Initial assessment.</p>'}
      <p>${mins} minutes this week · ${(n => n + " day" + (n === 1 ? "" : "s"))(new Set(hist.map(h => dayKey(h.at))).size)} practised${g ? " · plan: " + PLANS[g][0] : ""}</p>
      ${heatHTML(heatData(), true)}
      <details class="weekly"><summary><b>This week</b> (a short note you can share)</summary><pre class="wnote">${stEsc(weeklyNote(k))}</pre>
        <div class="row"><button class="ghost" type="button" data-share="${stEsc(k.id)}">Share or copy</button><a class="ghost linkbtn" href="mailto:?subject=${encodeURIComponent("This week in Mind the Gap 11+")}&body=${encodeURIComponent(weeklyNote(k))}">Email it</a></div></details>
      <div class="row"><button class="ghost" type="button" data-open="${stEsc(k.id)}">Open full progress and plan</button></div></div>`;
  })).join("") + `<button class="ghost" id="kidAdd" type="button">Add another child</button>`;
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-open]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => switchChild(b.dataset.open));
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-share]").forEach(b => b.onclick = async () => {
    const k = children().find(c => c.id === b.dataset.share) || { id: b.dataset.share }, text = weeklyNote(k);
    try { if (navigator.share) { await navigator.share({ title: "This week in Mind the Gap 11+", text }); return; } } catch (e) { return; }
    try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text); b.textContent = "Copied"; } catch (e) { b.textContent = "Select the note and copy it"; }
  });
  $("kidAdd").onclick = () => {
    const nm = prompt("Child's name"); if (!nm || !nm.trim()) return;
    const ks = children().slice(); if (!ks.some(c => c.id === CHILD)) ks.unshift({ id: CHILD, name: childName() });
    const id = "k" + Date.now().toString(36); ks.push({ id, name: nm.trim().slice(0, 30) }); store.set("children", ks); renderKids();
  };
}
function renderProfile() {
  const box = $("profBox"), kids = children(), me = kids.find(c => c.id === CHILD) || kids[0], plan = store.get("plan") || {};
  const lv = stDefaultLevel(), map = stMap(lv), res = profResults(), hist = getHistory(), { n } = streakDays();
  const week = hist.filter(h => h.at > Date.now() - 7 * 864e5), mins = Math.round(week.reduce((a, h) => a + (h.secs || 0), 0) / 60);
  const days = new Set(hist.map(h => dayKey(h.at))).size, due = dueReviews().length, missed = Object.keys(store.get("review") || {}).length;
  const ready = res.filter(r => r.total).slice(0, 3), readiness = ready.length ? Math.round(ready.reduce((a, r) => a + r.marks / r.total, 0) / ready.length * 100) : null;
  const tr = profSkillTrend(lv), d = t => new Date(t).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
  box.innerHTML = `<div class="prof" style="display:grid;gap:12px">
    ${kids.length > 1 ? `<div class="kids" role="group" aria-label="Choose a child">${kids.map(c => `<button type="button" class="ghost" data-kid="${stEsc(c.id)}" aria-pressed="${c.id === CHILD}">${avatarHTML(c, "kidav")}${stEsc(c.name || "Child 1")}</button>`).join("")}</div>` : ""}
    <h2>My progress</h2>
    <p class="muted">Year ${YEAR}${plan.testDate ? " · test on " + new Date(plan.testDate).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" }) + " (" + Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(plan.testDate) - Date.now()) / (7 * 864e5))) + " weeks to go)" : ""}</p>
    ${profPredict(res)}
    <div class="stats"><div class="stat"><b>${n}</b>day${n === 1 ? "" : "s"} in a row</div><div class="stat"><b>${mins}</b>minutes this week</div><div class="stat"><b>${days}</b>days practised</div><div class="stat"><b>${missed}</b>mistakes to review</div></div>
    ${due ? `<button class="primary" id="profMistakes" type="button">Review my mistakes (${due} due)</button>` : ""}
    <h3>Topic scores</h3>
    <p class="muted small">Every topic and part, from the Initial assessment and practice. Tap a square to practise it.</p>
    ${heatHTML(heatData())}${HEAT_LEGEND}
    <h3>This week's plan</h3>
    ${weekPlanHTML(weekPlan())}
    ${map ? `<details><summary><strong>Initial assessment results</strong></summary>` : ""}
    ${map ? stMapHTML(map).replace(/<strong>([^<]+)<\/strong>/g, (m, nm) => { const sk = stSkills(lv).find(s => s.name === nm.replace(/&amp;/g, "&")); return m + (sk ? tr(sk.id) : ""); }) : `<p class="muted">No results yet. Do the Initial assessment to see your strong and weak skills.</p><button class="primary" id="profStart" type="button">Start the Initial assessment</button>`}
    ${map ? "</details>" : ""}
    <h3>Results</h3>
    ${profTrendSVG(res)}
    ${res.length ? `<table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Test</th><th>Score</th><th>Time</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${res.slice(0, 30).map((r, i) => `<tr><td>${d(r.at)}</td><td>${stEsc(r.name)}</td><td>${r.marks} / ${r.total}${r.total ? " (" + Math.round(r.marks / r.total * 100) + "%)" : ""}</td><td>${r.secs ? fmtTime(r.secs) : ""}</td><td>${r.att ? `<button class="ghost" type="button" data-rev="${i}">Answers</button>` : ""}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table>` : `<p class="muted">No tests yet. Results from the Initial assessment, practice tests and mock tests appear here.</p>`}
    <details><summary><strong>Settings for this child</strong> (username, picture, test date, plan, colours, text size)</summary>
      <div style="display:grid;gap:10px;margin-top:10px">
        <label>Username <input id="pfName" type="text" value="${stEsc(me.name || "")}" maxlength="20"></label><button class="ghost" id="pfLook" type="button">Picture, colours and text size</button>
        <label>Test date <input id="pfDate" type="date" value="${plan.testDate || ""}"></label>
        <label>Plan <select id="pfPlan"><option value="">Automatic, from results${planAuto() ? " (" + PLANS[planAuto()][0] + ")" : ""}</option>${Object.entries(PLANS).map(([k, v]) => `<option value="${k}" ${plan.group === k ? "selected" : ""}>${v[0]}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
        <label>Minutes a day <select id="pfMins">${[10, 15, 20, 30].map(m => `<option ${(plan.mins || 20) === m ? "selected" : ""}>${m}</option>`).join("")}</select></label>
        <div class="row"><button class="primary" id="pfSave" type="button">Save</button><button class="ghost" id="pfAdd" type="button">Add another child</button></div>
        <div id="pfTheme">${themeHTML()}</div>
      </div></details></div>`;
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-kid]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => switchChild(b.dataset.kid));
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-rev]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const r = res[+b.dataset.rev]; ST.level = r.att.level; show("starterView"); stResult(r.att); });
  if (map) stWireMap(box);
  box.querySelectorAll(".hcell[data-topic]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { topic = b.dataset.topic; type = b.dataset.type; level = AUTO; start(); });
  const rtp = () => { $("pfTheme").innerHTML = themeHTML(); wireTheme($("pfTheme"), rtp); }; wireTheme($("pfTheme"), rtp);
  const ps = $("profStart"); if (ps) ps.onclick = stOpen;
  const pm = $("profMistakes"); if (pm) pm.onclick = () => startMistakes(dueReviews().map(r => ({ ...r.q })));
  $("pfSave").onclick = () => {
    const ks = children().slice(); let k = ks.find(c => c.id === CHILD); if (!k) { k = { id: CHILD, name: "" }; ks.unshift(k); }
    k.name = $("pfName").value.trim(); store.set("children", ks);
    store.set("plan", { ...plan, testDate: $("pfDate").value || null, mins: +$("pfMins").value, group: $("pfPlan").value || null });
    renderProfile(); renderProfileBtn();
  };
  $("pfLook").onclick = openMe;
  $("pfAdd").onclick = () => {
    const nm = prompt("Child's name"); if (!nm || !nm.trim()) return;
    const ks = children().slice(); if (!ks.some(c => c.id === CHILD)) ks.unshift({ id: CHILD, name: me.name || "" });
    const id = "k" + Date.now().toString(36); ks.push({ id, name: nm.trim().slice(0, 30) }); store.set("children", ks); switchChild(id);
  };
}



/* ----- cloud: Supabase, used only when config.js has the keys (parents sign in with an email link; no passwords, no child accounts) ----- */
const Cloud = (() => {
  const U = (CFG.supabaseUrl || "").replace(/\/$/, ""), K = CFG.supabaseKey || "", on = !!(U && K);
  let ses = null; try { ses = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("mtg:session")); } catch (e) {}
  const keep = x => { ses = x; try { x ? localStorage.setItem("mtg:session", JSON.stringify(x)) : localStorage.removeItem("mtg:session"); } catch (e) {} };
  const H = auth => ({ apikey: K, "Content-Type": "application/json", Authorization: "Bearer " + (auth && ses ? ses.access_token : K) });
  async function refresh() {
    if (!ses || !ses.refresh_token) return false;
    const r = await fetch(U + "/auth/v1/token?grant_type=refresh_token", { method: "POST", headers: H(false), body: JSON.stringify({ refresh_token: ses.refresh_token }) });
    if (!r.ok) { keep(null); return false; }
    const d = await r.json(); keep({ access_token: d.access_token, refresh_token: d.refresh_token, exp: Date.now() + (d.expires_in || 3600) * 1000, uid: d.user && d.user.id, email: d.user && d.user.email }); return true;
  }
  async function api(path, opt = {}, again) {
    if (ses && ses.exp && ses.exp < Date.now() + 60000) await refresh();
    const r = await fetch(U + path, { ...opt, headers: { ...H(true), ...(opt.headers || {}) } });
    if (r.status === 401 && !again && await refresh()) return api(path, opt, true);
    return r;
  }
  function fromLink() {   /* the sign-in link comes back with the session in the address */
    const h = new URLSearchParams(location.hash.slice(1));
    if (!h.get("access_token")) return false;
    keep({ access_token: h.get("access_token"), refresh_token: h.get("refresh_token"), exp: Date.now() + (+h.get("expires_in") || 3600) * 1000 });
    try { history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname); } catch (e) {}
    return true;
  }
  async function who() {
    if (!ses) return null;
    if (!ses.uid) { const r = await api("/auth/v1/user"); if (!r.ok) return null; const u = await r.json(); keep({ ...ses, uid: u.id, email: u.email }); }
    return ses;
  }
  async function sendLink(email) {
    const r = await fetch(U + "/auth/v1/otp", { method: "POST", headers: H(false), body: JSON.stringify({ email, create_user: true, options: { email_redirect_to: location.origin + location.pathname } }) });
    return r.ok;
  }
  /* everything the app keeps (all children) goes up as one family record */
  const mine = k => k.startsWith("y4maths:") && !/^y4maths:(access|cloudAccess)$/.test(k);
  function snapshot() {   /* children's photos stay on the device; everything else is saved to the parent's account */
    const o = {}; for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) { const k = localStorage.key(i); if (mine(k)) o[k] = localStorage.getItem(k); }
    Object.keys(o).filter(k => /(^|:)children$/.test(k)).forEach(k => { try { o[k] = JSON.stringify((JSON.parse(o[k]) || []).map(c => ({ ...c, photo: null }))); } catch (e) {} });
    return o;
  }
  function merge(remote) {
    Object.entries(remote || {}).forEach(([k, v]) => {
      if (!mine(k)) return;
      const local = localStorage.getItem(k);
      if (local == null) { localStorage.setItem(k, v); return; }
      if (/(^|:)history$/.test(k)) {   /* keep every session from both devices */
        try { const a = JSON.parse(local) || [], b = JSON.parse(v) || [], seen = new Set(), all = [];
          [...a, ...b].sort((x, y) => x.at - y.at).forEach(h => { if (!seen.has(h.at)) { seen.add(h.at); all.push(h); } });
          localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(all.slice(-300))); } catch (e) {}
      }
    });
  }
  let timer = null, busy = false;
  async function push() {
    if (!on || !ses || busy) return; busy = true;
    try { const me = await who(); if (me) await api("/rest/v1/family", { method: "POST", headers: { Prefer: "resolution=merge-duplicates" }, body: JSON.stringify({ user_id: me.uid, data: snapshot(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }) }); }
    catch (e) {} finally { busy = false; }
  }
  window.__cloudDirty = () => { if (!on || !ses) return; clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(push, 4000); };
  async function pull() {
    const me = await who(); if (!me) return false;
    try {
      const r = await api(`/rest/v1/family?user_id=eq.${me.uid}&select=data`); if (r.ok) { const rows = await r.json(); if (rows[0]) merge(rows[0].data); }
      const a = await api(`/rest/v1/access?user_id=eq.${me.uid}&select=plan,until`); if (a.ok) { const rows = await a.json(); localStorage.setItem("y4maths:cloudAccess", JSON.stringify(rows[0] || null)); }
    } catch (e) { return false; }
    push(); return true;
  }
  const queue = [];
  function bump(qid, wrong, report) {   /* shared, anonymous: only the question id and whether it was missed */
    if (!on || !qid) return; queue.push({ qid: String(qid), wrong: !!wrong, report: !!report });
    if (queue.length === 1) setTimeout(async () => { const batch = queue.splice(0); try { await fetch(U + "/rest/v1/rpc/bump_questions", { method: "POST", headers: H(false), body: JSON.stringify({ items: batch }) }); } catch (e) {} }, 8000);
  }
  function signOut() { keep(null); try { localStorage.removeItem("y4maths:cloudAccess"); } catch (e) {} }
  async function wipe() { const me = await who(); if (me) await api(`/rest/v1/family?user_id=eq.${me.uid}`, { method: "DELETE" }); signOut(); }
  return { on, wipe, fromLink, who, sendLink, pull, push, bump, signOut, get email() { return ses && ses.email; }, get signedIn() { return !!ses; }, get uid() { return ses && ses.uid; } };
})();
/* phone keyboard: when it covers the screen, switch to the compact layout above */
(() => {
  const vv = window.visualViewport; if (!vv) return;
  const typing = () => { const a = document.activeElement; return a && a.tagName === "INPUT" && !/^(checkbox|radio|button|range|color|file)$/.test(a.type) && a.closest("#quiz, #starterView, #raceView"); };
  const full = () => matchMedia("(orientation: landscape)").matches ? Math.min(screen.width, screen.height) : Math.max(screen.width, screen.height);
  let was = false;
  const check = () => {
    const on = !!typing() && vv.height < full() * 0.72;
    document.body.classList.toggle("kb", on);
    if (on && !was) setTimeout(() => { const a = document.activeElement, q = $("qtext");
      if (q && !q.closest("[hidden]") && a.closest("#quiz")) q.scrollIntoView({ block: "start" }); a.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" }); }, 60);
    was = on;
  };
  vv.addEventListener("resize", check); addEventListener("focusin", () => setTimeout(check, 50)); addEventListener("focusout", () => setTimeout(check, 50));
})();
if ("serviceWorker" in navigator && location.protocol === "https:" && !/claude|anthropic/.test(location.hostname)) addEventListener("load", () => navigator.serviceWorker.register("sw.js").catch(() => {}));
if (Cloud.on) (async () => { const back = Cloud.fromLink(); if (Cloud.signedIn && await Cloud.pull() && back) location.reload(); })();
/* ----- learning from real use: how often each question is got wrong ----- */
function qStat(id, ok) {
  if (!id || String(id).startsWith("gen")) return;
  const st = store.get("qstats") || {}; const r = st[id] || [0, 0]; r[0]++; if (!ok) r[1]++; st[id] = r; store.set("qstats", st);
  if (typeof Cloud !== "undefined") Cloud.bump(id, !ok);
}
function hardQuestions() {   /* questions tried 3+ times and missed 70%+ of the time on this device */
  const st = store.get("qstats") || {};
  return Object.entries(st).filter(([, r]) => r[0] >= 3 && r[1] / r[0] >= 0.7).sort((a, b) => b[1][1] / b[1][0] - a[1][1] / a[1][0]).slice(0, 40)
    .map(([id, r]) => ({ id, tries: r[0], wrong: r[1], q: QUESTIONS.find(q => q.id === id) })).filter(x => x.q);
}
/* ----- plans and access -----
   PAYWALL false: everything is free while the app is tested. To charge, create two Stripe Payment Links,
   put them in PAY_LINKS and set PAYWALL to true. Codes give full access on this device (stored as SHA-256 hashes). */
const PAYWALL = !!CFG.paywall;
const PAY_LINKS = { monthly: CFG.payMonthly || "", oneoff: CFG.payOneoff || "" };
const FREE = { papers: 2, sessionsPerDay: 3 };
const CODE_HASHES = ["e9837c8bc91ebbb254c93a20b1110f6f3428ed265423166d0fb0c92389823940", "62fba173617763b00cb3d5b8a4e31c4e07c0ba4538b7743639fbc020c118e2fe", "20f745b1197959bbd232d3bb9a73523a454e5b9ae8a3fe39eabfa948eb0adb2d"];
const access = () => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("y4maths:access")) || null; } catch (e) { return null; } };
const cloudAccess = () => { try { const a = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("y4maths:cloudAccess")); return a && a.until && new Date(a.until) > new Date() ? a : null; } catch (e) { return null; } };
const hasFull = () => !PAYWALL || !!access() || !!cloudAccess();
function sessionsToday() { const d = dayKey(Date.now()); return getHistory().filter(h => dayKey(h.at) === d && !h.mock).length; }
async function sha256(t) { const b = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(t)); return [...new Uint8Array(b)].map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(""); }
function renderPlan() {
  const a = access(), box = $("pPlan"), d = t => new Date(t).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" });
  const card = (k, title, price, lines) => `<div class="kidcard"><h3>${title}</h3><p style="font-size:1.4rem;margin:0"><b>${price}</b></p><ul>${lines.map(l => `<li>${l}</li>`).join("")}</ul>
    ${k ? `<button class="primary" type="button" data-buy="${k}">Choose</button>` : ""}</div>`;
  box.innerHTML = `<div style="display:grid;gap:12px">
    <p>${cloudAccess() ? `<b>Full access</b> until ${d(cloudAccess().until)} (${stEsc(cloudAccess().plan || "paid")}).` : a ? `<b>Full access</b> on this device since ${d(a.at)}${a.kind === "code" ? " (access code)" : ""}.` : PAYWALL ? "<b>Free plan</b> on this device." : "<b>Everything is free</b> while the app is being tested."}</p>
    ${card("", "Free", "£0", ["Initial assessment A and B, with results for every topic", `Practice papers 1 and ${FREE.papers}`, `${FREE.sessionsPerDay} topic practice sessions a day`])}
    ${card("monthly", "Monthly", "£6 a month", ["Everything in the app for one child", "All 50 practice papers and every topic", "Cancel any time"])}
    ${card("oneoff", "Until the test", "£39 once", ["Everything in the app for one child", "Lasts until the end of September of the test year", "No subscription"])}
    <p class="muted small">Brothers and sisters: each extra child is half price.</p>
    <h3>Your account</h3><div id="acctBox"></div>
    <p class="muted small"><a href="/privacy.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Privacy policy</a> · we keep as little about children as we can, and never sell or share it.</p>
    <p class="muted" id="planMsg"></p>
    <h3>Have an access code?</h3>
    <div class="row"><input id="codeIn" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="Access code" style="max-width:220px;font:inherit;padding:10px 12px;border:2px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink)"><button class="ghost" id="codeGo" type="button">Use code</button></div></div>`;
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-buy]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {
    const url = PAY_LINKS[b.dataset.buy];
    if (!url) { $("planMsg").textContent = PAYWALL ? "Payments are not set up yet." : "Payments are not switched on yet: everything is free for now."; return; }
    if (Cloud.on && !Cloud.uid) { $("planMsg").textContent = "Sign in below first, so the payment is linked to your account on every device."; return; }
    location.href = url + (url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?") + "client_reference_id=" + encodeURIComponent(Cloud.uid || "") + (Cloud.email ? "&prefilled_email=" + encodeURIComponent(Cloud.email) : "");
  });
  const acc = $("acctBox");
  if (acc) {
    if (!Cloud.on) acc.innerHTML = '<p class="muted small">Parent accounts are not switched on yet, so progress is kept on this device only.</p>';
    else if (Cloud.signedIn) {
      acc.innerHTML = `<p>Signed in as <b>${stEsc(Cloud.email || "")}</b>. Progress for all your children is saved to your account and appears on any device where you sign in.</p><div class="row"><button class="ghost" id="acSync" type="button">Sync now</button><button class="ghost" id="acOut" type="button">Sign out</button><button class="ghost" id="acDel" type="button">Delete my saved data</button></div>`;
      $("acDel").onclick = async () => { if (!confirm("Delete everything saved in your account? Progress on this device stays.")) return; await Cloud.wipe(); renderPlan(); };
      $("acSync").onclick = async () => { $("acSync").textContent = "Syncing…"; await Cloud.pull(); $("acSync").textContent = "Synced"; };
      $("acOut").onclick = () => { Cloud.signOut(); renderPlan(); };
    } else {
      acc.innerHTML = `<p class="muted small">Sign in to save progress to your account and use the app on more than one device. We email you a sign-in link: no password.</p>
        <div class="row"><input id="acMail" type="email" autocomplete="email" placeholder="Parent's email" style="max-width:260px;font:inherit;padding:10px 12px;border:2px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink)"><button class="primary" id="acGo" type="button">Email me a link</button></div><p class="muted small" id="acMsg"></p>`;
      $("acGo").onclick = async () => { const e = $("acMail").value.trim(); if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(e)) { $("acMsg").textContent = "Please enter an email address."; return; }
        $("acMsg").textContent = (await Cloud.sendLink(e)) ? "Check your email and tap the link on this device." : "That didn't work. Please try again in a minute."; };
    }
  }
  $("codeGo").onclick = async () => {
    const c = $("codeIn").value.trim().toUpperCase(); if (!c) return;
    let ok = false; try { ok = CODE_HASHES.includes(await sha256(c)); } catch (e) {}
    if (ok) { try { localStorage.setItem("y4maths:access", JSON.stringify({ kind: "code", at: Date.now() })); } catch (e) {} renderPlan(); }
    else $("planMsg").textContent = "That code didn't work. Check the spelling and try again.";
  };
}

function kidSVG(k) {
  const c = KIDS[k], back = { long: `<path d="M24 50 Q22 18 50 17 Q78 18 76 50 L79 84 L21 84 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/>`, afro: `<circle cx="50" cy="42" r="28" fill="${c.hair}"/>`,
    braids: `<path d="M27 46 Q26 18 50 18 Q74 18 73 46 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/><rect x="22" y="44" width="8" height="34" rx="4" fill="${c.hair}"/><rect x="70" y="44" width="8" height="34" rx="4" fill="${c.hair}"/>`,
    bob: `<path d="M25 58 Q22 18 50 18 Q78 18 75 58 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/>`, hijab: `<path d="M22 100 Q20 60 26 44 Q32 18 50 17 Q68 18 74 44 Q80 60 78 100 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/>` }[c.style] || "";
  const top = { short: `<path d="M29 46 Q29 22 50 21 Q71 22 71 46 Q66 32 50 32 Q36 32 29 46 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/>`, curly: `<g fill="${c.hair}"><circle cx="34" cy="33" r="8"/><circle cx="44" cy="27" r="8"/><circle cx="56" cy="27" r="8"/><circle cx="66" cy="33" r="8"/></g>`,
    long: `<path d="M30 42 Q34 26 50 26 Q64 26 70 42 Q60 33 50 34 Q40 33 30 42 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/>`, bob: `<path d="M30 40 Q36 27 50 27 Q66 27 70 40 Q58 34 30 40 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/>`,
    braids: "", afro: "", hijab: "", patka: `<path d="M29 42 Q29 22 50 21 Q71 22 71 42 Q60 36 50 36 Q40 36 29 42 Z" fill="${c.hair}"/><circle cx="50" cy="19" r="7" fill="${c.hair}"/>` }[c.style] || "";
  const face = c.style === "hijab" ? `<ellipse cx="50" cy="50" rx="15" ry="18" fill="${c.skin}"/>` : `<circle cx="50" cy="49" r="20" fill="${c.skin}"/>`;
  const glasses = c.glasses ? `<g fill="none" stroke="#222" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="43" cy="49" r="5.5"/><circle cx="57" cy="49" r="5.5"/><path d="M48.5 49 h3"/></g>` : "";
  return [c.bg, `${back}<path d="M18 104 Q20 76 50 74 Q80 76 82 104 Z" fill="${c.style === "hijab" ? c.hair : c.shirt}"/><rect x="44" y="64" width="12" height="12" fill="${c.skin}"/>${face}${top}
    <circle cx="43" cy="49" r="2.3" fill="#222"/><circle cx="57" cy="49" r="2.3" fill="#222"/><path d="M43 57 Q50 62 57 57" stroke="#7a3b2e" stroke-width="2" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/>${glasses}`];
}
{ const animals = { ...AVATARS }; Object.keys(AVATARS).forEach(k => delete AVATARS[k]); Object.keys(KIDS).forEach(k => { AVATARS[k] = kidSVG(k); }); Object.assign(AVATARS, animals); }   /* children first, then animals */
let AVCLIP = 0;
const meKid = () => children().find(c => c.id === CHILD) || children()[0] || { id: CHILD, name: "" };
function avatarHTML(k, cls) {
  k = k || meKid(); cls = cls || "avatar";
  if (k.photo) return `<img class="${cls}" src="${k.photo}" alt="">`;
  const key = AVATARS[k.avatar] ? k.avatar : null;
  if (!key) { const ch = (k.name || "?").trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "?"; return `<svg class="${cls}" viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="50" cy="50" r="50" fill="var(--accent)"/><text x="50" y="64" text-anchor="middle" font-size="44" font-weight="700" fill="var(--accent-ink)">${stEsc(ch)}</text></svg>`; }
  const [bg, art] = AVATARS[key], id = "avc" + (++AVCLIP);
  return `<svg class="${cls}" viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden="true"><defs><clipPath id="${id}"><circle cx="50" cy="50" r="50"/></clipPath></defs><g clip-path="url(#${id})"><circle cx="50" cy="50" r="50" fill="${bg}"/>${art}</g></svg>`;
}
function saveKid(change) {
  const ks = children().slice(); let k = ks.find(c => c.id === CHILD); if (!k) { k = { id: CHILD, name: "" }; ks.unshift(k); }
  Object.assign(k, change); store.set("children", ks); renderMeBtn(); if (typeof renderProfileBtn === "function") renderProfileBtn();
}
function renderMeBtn() {   /* "My profile", with the child's picture once one is chosen */
  const k = meKid();
  $("meBtn").innerHTML = (k.photo || AVATARS[k.avatar] ? avatarHTML(k, "avatar mini") : "") + "<span>My profile</span>";
}
function photoFrom(file, done) {   /* shrink to 160 px so it is small to keep */
  const r = new FileReader();
  r.onload = () => { const img = new Image(); img.onload = () => { const c = document.createElement("canvas"), z = 160, m = Math.min(img.width, img.height); c.width = c.height = z;
    c.getContext("2d").drawImage(img, (img.width - m) / 2, (img.height - m) / 2, m, m, 0, 0, z, z); done(c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.8)); }; img.src = r.result; };
  r.readAsDataURL(file);
}
function openMe() {
  const k = meKid(), kids = children(), box = $("meBox");
  box.innerHTML = `<div style="display:grid;gap:14px">
    <div class="mehead">${avatarHTML(k, "avatar big")}<div><h2 id="meTitle" style="margin:0">${k.name ? stEsc(k.name) : "My profile"}</h2><span class="muted small">Your name, picture and how the app looks</span></div></div>
    <label>Username <input id="meName" type="text" maxlength="20" value="${stEsc(k.name || "")}" placeholder="e.g. MathsFox"></label>
    <div><b>Picture</b><div class="av-pick" role="group" aria-label="Choose a picture" style="margin-top:8px">${Object.keys(AVATARS).map((a, i) => `<button type="button" data-av="${a}" aria-label="${KIDS[a] ? "Child picture " + (i + 1) : a}" aria-pressed="${!k.photo && k.avatar === a}">${avatarHTML({ avatar: a })}</button>`).join("")}</div>
      <div class="row" style="margin-top:10px"><label class="ghost" style="display:inline-block;padding:8px 14px;border:2px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer">Use a photo<input id="mePhoto" type="file" accept="image/*" hidden></label>${k.photo ? '<button class="ghost" id="meNoPhoto" type="button">Remove photo</button>' : ""}</div></div>
    <div id="meTheme">${themeHTML()}</div>
    ${kids.length > 1 ? `<div><b>Switch child</b><div class="row" style="margin-top:8px">${kids.filter(c => c.id !== CHILD).map(c => `<button type="button" class="ghost" data-kid="${stEsc(c.id)}">${avatarHTML(c, "kidav")}${stEsc(c.name || "Child")}</button>`).join("")}</div></div>` : ""}
    <div class="row"><button class="primary" id="meDone" type="button">Done</button></div></div>`;
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-av]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { saveKid({ avatar: b.dataset.av, photo: null }); openMe(); });
  $("mePhoto").onchange = e => { const f = e.target.files[0]; if (f) photoFrom(f, url => { saveKid({ photo: url }); openMe(); }); };
  const np = $("meNoPhoto"); if (np) np.onclick = () => { saveKid({ photo: null }); openMe(); };
  $("meName").onchange = () => saveKid({ name: $("meName").value.trim().slice(0, 20) });
  const rt = () => { $("meTheme").innerHTML = themeHTML(); wireTheme($("meTheme"), rt); }; wireTheme($("meTheme"), rt);
  box.querySelectorAll("[data-kid]").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => switchChild(b.dataset.kid));
  $("meDone").onclick = () => { saveKid({ name: $("meName").value.trim().slice(0, 20) }); $("meDlg").close(); };
  if (!$("meDlg").open) $("meDlg").showModal();
}
$("meBtn").onclick = openMe;
renderMeBtn();
